==================================================
FILE: project-inventory.ps1
==================================================

$ErrorActionPreference = "SilentlyContinue"

$root = Get-Location
$output = Join-Path $root "EVENTEASE_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"

$excludeDirs = @(
    "node_modules",
    ".git",
    "dist",
    "build",
    ".vite",
    "coverage"
)

$excludeFiles = @(
    ".env",
    ".env.local",
    ".env.production",
    ".env.development",
    "package-lock.json",
    "yarn.lock",
    "pnpm-lock.yaml"
)

$includeExtensions = @(
    ".js", ".jsx", ".ts", ".tsx",
    ".css", ".scss",
    ".json", ".html",
    ".md", ".txt",
    ".ps1"
)

"EVENTEASE PROJECT SNAPSHOT" | Set-Content $output
"Generated: $(Get-Date)" | Add-Content $output
"Project root: $root" | Add-Content $output

"`n========== PROJECT FILE TREE ==========" | Add-Content $output

$files = Get-ChildItem -Path $root -Recurse -File | Where-Object {
    $relative = $_.FullName.Substring($root.Path.Length).TrimStart("\")
    $parts = $relative -split "\\"

    ($parts | Where-Object { $excludeDirs -contains $_ }).Count -eq 0 -and
    ($excludeFiles -notcontains $_.Name) -and
    ($includeExtensions -contains $_.Extension)
}

foreach ($file in $files) {
    $relative = $file.FullName.Substring($root.Path.Length).TrimStart("\")
    $relative | Add-Content $output
}

"`n========== FILE CONTENTS ==========" | Add-Content $output

foreach ($file in $files) {
    $relative = $file.FullName.Substring($root.Path.Length).TrimStart("\")

    "`n`n==================================================" | Add-Content $output
    "FILE: $relative" | Add-Content $output
    "==================================================" | Add-Content $output

    if ($file.Length -gt 300000) {
        "[SKIPPED: File larger than 300 KB]" | Add-Content $output
        continue
    }

    if ($file.Length -eq 0) {
        "[EMPTY FILE]" | Add-Content $output
        continue
    }

    Get-Content -LiteralPath $file.FullName -Raw | Add-Content $output
}

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "Project inventory generated successfully!" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "Output: $output" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Files included: $($files.Count)"

FILE: backend\seedAdmin.js
==================================================

require("dotenv").config();

const mongoose = require("mongoose");
const User = require("./src/models/User");

const seedAdmin = async () => {
  try {
    await mongoose.connect(process.env.MONGO_URI);

    const email = process.env.ADMIN_EMAIL;

    const existingAdmin = await User.findOne({ email });

    if (existingAdmin) {
      console.log("Admin account already exists.");
      return;
    }

    await User.create({
      name: process.env.ADMIN_NAME,
      email,
      password: process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD,
      role: "admin"
    });

    console.log("EventEase Admin created successfully.");
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Admin creation failed:", error.message);
    process.exitCode = 1;
  } finally {
    await mongoose.disconnect();
  }
};

seedAdmin();

const app = require("./src/app");
const connectDB = require("./src/config/db");

const PORT = process.env.PORT || 5000;

const startServer = async () => {
  await connectDB();

  app.listen(PORT, () => {
    console.log(`EventEase server running at http://localhost:${PORT}`);
  });
};

startServer();

FILE: backend\src\app.js

const express = require("express");
const path = require("path");
const cors = require("cors");
const helmet = require("helmet");
const morgan = require("morgan");
const rateLimit = require("express-rate-limit");

const authRoutes = require("./routes/auth.routes");
const adminRoutes = require("./routes/admin.routes");
const eventRoutes = require("./routes/event.routes");
const adminEventRoutes = require("./routes/adminEvent.routes");
const bookingRoutes = require("./routes/booking.routes");
const notificationRoutes = require("./routes/notification.routes");
const userRoutes = require("./routes/user.routes");

const app = express();

// Serve uploaded event posters and other uploaded files
app.use(
  "/uploads",
  express.static(path.join(__dirname, "../uploads"))
);

app.use(helmet());

app.use(cors({
  origin: process.env.CLIENT_URL || "http://localhost:5173",
  credentials: true
}));

app.use(express.json({ limit: "10kb" }));
app.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true }));
app.use(morgan("dev"));

// API rate limiting
app.use("/api", rateLimit({
  windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000,
  limit: 200,
  standardHeaders: "draft-8",
  legacyHeaders: false
}));

// Health check
app.get("/health", (req, res) => {
  res.status(200).json({
    success: true,
    message: "EventEase API is running!",
    status: "healthy"
  });
});

// Authentication routes
app.use("/api/auth", authRoutes);

// Admin routes
app.use("/api/admin", adminRoutes);

// Event routes
app.use("/api/events", eventRoutes);

// Admin event routes
app.use("/api/admin/events", adminEventRoutes);

// Booking routes
app.use("/api/bookings", bookingRoutes);

// Notification routes
app.use("/api/notifications", notificationRoutes);

// User routes
app.use("/api/users", userRoutes);

// 404 handler
app.use((req, res) => {
  res.status(404).json({
    success: false,
    message: "API route not found"
  });
});

module.exports = app;

    const connection = await mongoose.connect(process.env.MONGO_URI);

    console.log(`MongoDB connected: ${connection.connection.host}`);

    console.error("MongoDB connection failed:", error.message);
    process.exit(1);
  }
};

module.exports = connectDB;

FILE: backend\src\controllers\admin.controller.js

const User = require("../models/User");

// View all users
exports.getUsers = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const users = await User.find()
      .select("-password")
      .sort({ createdAt: -1 });

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      count: users.length,
      users
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Could not retrieve users."
    });
  }
};

// View pending organizer applications
exports.getPendingOrganizers = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const users = await User.find({
      organizerStatus: "pending"
    }).select("-password");

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      count: users.length,
      applications: users
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Could not retrieve applications."
    });
  }
};

// Approve or reject organizer
exports.reviewOrganizer = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { action } = req.body;

    if (!["approve", "reject"].includes(action)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Action must be approve or reject."
      });
    }

    const user = await User.findOne({
      _id: req.params.id,
      organizerStatus: "pending"
    });

    if (!user) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Pending application not found."
      });
    }

    user.organizerStatus =
      action === "approve" ? "approved" : "rejected";

    if (action === "approve") {
      user.role = "organizer";
    }

    await user.save();

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: `Organizer application ${action}d successfully.`,
      user: {
        id: user._id,
        name: user.name,
        email: user.email,
        role: user.role,
        organizerStatus: user.organizerStatus
      }
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Could not review application."
    });
  }
};



==================================================
FILE: backend\src\controllers\adminEvent.controller.js
==================================================

const mongoose = require("mongoose");
const Event = require("../models/Event");
const Notification = require("../models/Notification");

// Get all events awaiting admin approval
exports.getPendingEvents = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const events = await Event.find({ approvalStatus: "pending" })
      .populate("organizer", "name email")
      .sort({ createdAt: -1 });

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      count: events.length,
      events
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Get pending events error:", error);

    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Failed to fetch pending events."
    });
  }
};

// Approve or reject an event
exports.reviewEvent = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { action, reason } = req.body;

    if (!mongoose.isValidObjectId(req.params.id)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid event ID."
      });
    }

    if (!["approve", "reject"].includes(action)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Action must be approve or reject."
      });
    }

    const event = await Event.findById(req.params.id);

    if (!event) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Event not found."
      });
    }

    if (event.approvalStatus !== "pending") {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "This event has already been reviewed."
      });
    }

    event.approvalStatus =
      action === "approve" ? "approved" : "rejected";

    await event.save();

    // Notify the organizer
    try {
      await Notification.create({
        recipient: event.organizer,
        title: action === "approve"
          ? "Event approved"
          : "Event rejected",
        message: action === "approve"
          ? `Your event "${event.title}" has been approved.`
          : `Your event "${event.title}" was rejected. ${reason || ""}`,
        type: "event"
      });
    } catch (notificationError) {
      console.error("Notification creation failed:", notificationError);
    }

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: `Event ${action === "approve" ? "approved" : "rejected"} successfully.`,
      event
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Review event error:", error);

    return res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Failed to review event."
    });
  }
};

FILE: backend\src\controllers\auth.controller.js
==================================================

const User = require("../models/User");
const jwt = require("jsonwebtoken");

const generateToken = (userId) => {
  return jwt.sign(
    { id: userId },
    process.env.JWT_SECRET,
    { expiresIn: process.env.JWT_EXPIRES_IN || "7d" }
  );
};

// REGISTER
exports.register = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { name, email, password } = req.body;

    const existingUser = await User.findOne({ email });

    if (existingUser) {
      return res.status(409).json({
        success: false,
        message: "An account with this email already exists."
      });
    }

    const user = await User.create({
      name,
      email,
      password,
      role: "user"
    });

    const token = generateToken(user._id);

    res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: "Account created successfully!",
      token,
      user: {
        id: user._id,
        name: user.name,
        email: user.email,
        role: user.role
      }
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Registration failed.",
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// LOGIN
exports.login = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { email, password } = req.body;

    const user = await User.findOne({ email }).select("+password");

    if (!user || !(await user.comparePassword(password))) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid email or password."
      });
    }

    if (!user.isActive) {
      return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message: "This account has been deactivated."
      });
    }

    const token = generateToken(user._id);

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: "Login successful!",
      token,
      user: {
        id: user._id,
        name: user.name,
        email: user.email,
        role: user.role
      }
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Login failed.",
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// GET CURRENT USER
exports.getMe = async (req, res) => {
  res.status(200).json({
    success: true,
    user: req.user
  });
};

// LOGOUT
exports.logout = async (req, res) => {
  res.status(200).json({
    success: true,
    message: "Logout successful. Please clear the token on the client."
  });
};

// APPLY TO BECOME AN ORGANIZER
exports.applyOrganizer = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const user = await User.findById(req.user._id);

    if (user.role === "admin" || user.role === "organizer") {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Your account already has elevated access."
      });
    }

    if (user.organizerStatus === "pending") {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Your organizer application is already pending."
      });
    }

    user.organizerStatus = "pending";
    await user.save();

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: "Organizer application submitted successfully.",
      organizerStatus: user.organizerStatus
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Could not submit organizer application."
    });
  }
};

FILE: backend\src\controllers\booking.controller.js
==================================================

const Booking = require("../models/Booking");
const Event = require("../models/Event");
const Notification = require("../models/Notification");
const crypto = require("crypto");
const generateQR = require("../utils/generateQR");

// Book tickets
exports.createBooking = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { eventId, quantity = 1 } = req.body;
    const seats = Number(quantity);

    if (!eventId || !Number.isInteger(seats) || seats < 1 || seats > 10) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Provide a valid event ID and quantity (1-10)."
      });
    }

    const updatedEvent = await Event.findOneAndUpdate(
      {
        _id: eventId,
        approvalStatus: "approved",
        status: "upcoming",
        startDate: { $gt: new Date() },
        availableSeats: { $gte: seats }
      },
      { $inc: { availableSeats: -seats } },
      { new: true }
    );

    if (!updatedEvent) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Event unavailable or not enough tickets."
      });
    }

    try {
      const ticketCode = crypto.randomUUID();
      const qrCode = await generateQR(ticketCode);

      const booking = await Booking.create({
        event: eventId,
        attendee: req.user._id,
        quantity: seats,
        totalAmount: updatedEvent.ticketPrice * seats,
        bookingStatus: "confirmed",
        paymentStatus: "pending",
        ticketCode,
        qrCode
      });

      await Notification.create({
        recipient: req.user._id,
        title: "Booking confirmed",
        message: `You booked ${seats} ticket(s) for ${updatedEvent.title}.`,
        type: "booking"
      });

      return res.status(201).json({
        success: true,
        message: "Tickets booked successfully.",
        booking
      });
    } catch (error) {
      await Event.findByIdAndUpdate(eventId, {
        $inc: { availableSeats: seats }
      });
      throw error;
    }
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Booking failed.",
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// Get logged-in user's bookings
exports.getMyBookings = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const bookings = await Booking.find({
      attendee: req.user._id
    })
      .populate("event")
      .sort({ createdAt: -1 });

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      count: bookings.length,
      bookings
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Could not fetch bookings.",
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// Cancel a booking
exports.cancelBooking = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const booking = await Booking.findOne({
      _id: req.params.id,
      attendee: req.user._id,
      bookingStatus: "confirmed"
    });

    if (!booking) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Active booking not found."
      });
    }

    const event = await Event.findById(booking.event);

    if (!event || event.startDate <= new Date()) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Tickets cannot be cancelled after the event has started."
      });
    }

    booking.bookingStatus = "cancelled";

    if (booking.paymentStatus === "paid") {
      booking.paymentStatus = "refunded";
    }

    await booking.save();

    await Event.findByIdAndUpdate(event._id, {
      $inc: { availableSeats: booking.quantity }
    });

    await Notification.create({
      recipient: req.user._id,
      title: "Booking cancelled",
      message: `Your booking for ${event.title} has been cancelled.`,
      type: "booking"
    });

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: "Booking cancelled successfully.",
      booking
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Cancellation failed.",
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// Verify a ticket
exports.verifyTicket = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { ticketCode } = req.body;

    if (!ticketCode) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Ticket code is required."
      });
    }

    const booking = await Booking.findOne({
      ticketCode,
      bookingStatus: "confirmed"
    }).populate("event", "title organizer startDate");

    if (!booking) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid or cancelled ticket."
      });
    }

    if (
      booking.event.organizer.toString() !== req.user._id.toString() &&
      req.user.role !== "admin"
    ) {
      return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message: "You cannot verify tickets for this event."
      });
    }

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: "Ticket is valid.",
      ticket: {
        attendee: booking.attendee,
        event: booking.event.title,
        quantity: booking.quantity,
        ticketCode: booking.ticketCode
      }
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Ticket verification failed.",
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

FILE: backend\src\controllers\event.controller.js
==================================================

const Event = require("../models/Event");
const User = require("../models/User");

// Create a new event

exports.createEvent = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const user = await User.findById(req.user._id);

    if (
      !user ||
      user.role !== "organizer" ||
      user.organizerStatus !== "approved"
    ) {
      return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Only approved organizers can create events."
      });
    }

    const {
      title,
      description,
      category,
      tags,
      venue,
      location,
      startDate,
      endDate,
      ticketPrice,
      capacity
    } = req.body;

    if (
      !title ||
      !description ||
      !category ||
      !venue ||
      !location ||
      !startDate ||
      !endDate ||
      ticketPrice === undefined ||
      capacity === undefined
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Please provide all required event details."
      });
    }

    if (
      isNaN(Date.parse(startDate)) ||
      isNaN(Date.parse(endDate)) ||
      new Date(endDate) <= new Date(startDate)
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Please provide valid event dates."
      });
    }

    if (
      !Number.isFinite(Number(ticketPrice)) ||
      !Number.isInteger(Number(capacity)) ||
      Number(ticketPrice) < 0 ||
      Number(capacity) < 1
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid ticket price or capacity."
      });
    }

    // Parse tags sent as JSON from the frontend
    let parsedTags = [];

    if (tags) {
      try {
        parsedTags = Array.isArray(tags)
          ? tags
          : JSON.parse(tags);
      } catch {
        parsedTags = String(tags)
          .split(",")
          .map((tag) => tag.trim())
          .filter(Boolean);
      }
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(parsedTags)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Tags must be a valid list."
      });
    }

    const posterPath = req.file
      ? `/uploads/${req.file.filename}`
      : "";

    const event = await Event.create({
      title: title.trim(),
      description: description.trim(),
      category,
      tags: parsedTags,
      poster: posterPath,
      organizer: user._id,
      venue: venue.trim(),
      location: location.trim(),
      startDate,
      endDate,
      ticketPrice: Number(ticketPrice),
      capacity: Number(capacity),
      availableSeats: Number(capacity),
      approvalStatus: "pending"
    });

    res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: "Event submitted for admin approval.",
      event
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Create event error:", error);

    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Failed to create event.",
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};


// Get all approved events with search, filters and pagination
exports.getEvents = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const {
      search, category, location,
      sort = "date", page = 1, limit = 9
    } = req.query;

    const filter = {
      approvalStatus: "approved",
      status: { $ne: "cancelled" }
    };

    if (category) filter.category = category;

    if (location) {
      filter.location = { $regex: location, $options: "i" };
    }

    if (search) {
      filter.$text = { $search: search };
    }

    const sortOptions = {
      date: { startDate: 1 },
      newest: { createdAt: -1 },
      priceLow: { ticketPrice: 1 },
      priceHigh: { ticketPrice: -1 }
    };

    const currentPage = Math.max(1, Number(page) || 1);
    const pageLimit = Math.min(50, Math.max(1, Number(limit) || 9));

    const [events, total] = await Promise.all([
      Event.find(filter)
        .populate("organizer", "name")
        .sort(sortOptions[sort] || sortOptions.date)
        .skip((currentPage - 1) * pageLimit)
        .limit(pageLimit),
      Event.countDocuments(filter)
    ]);

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      count: events.length,
      total,
      page: currentPage,
      totalPages: Math.ceil(total / pageLimit),
      events
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Failed to fetch events.",
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// Get one approved event
exports.getEventById = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const event = await Event.findOne({
      _id: req.params.id,
      approvalStatus: "approved"
    }).populate("organizer", "name");

    if (!event) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Event not found."
      });
    }

    res.status(200).json({ success: true, event });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Failed to fetch event.",
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// Update an event owned by the organizer
exports.updateEvent = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const event = await Event.findOne({
      _id: req.params.id,
      organizer: req.user._id
    });

    if (!event) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Event not found or access denied."
      });
    }

    const allowedFields = [
      "title", "description", "category", "tags",
      "venue", "location", "startDate", "endDate",
      "ticketPrice", "capacity"
    ];

    const bookedSeats = event.capacity - event.availableSeats;

    allowedFields.forEach((field) => {
      if (req.body[field] !== undefined) {
        event[field] = req.body[field];
      }
    });

    if (
      isNaN(new Date(event.startDate).getTime()) ||
      isNaN(new Date(event.endDate).getTime()) ||
      new Date(event.endDate) <= new Date(event.startDate)
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid event dates."
      });
    }

    if (
      !Number.isFinite(Number(event.ticketPrice)) ||
      Number(event.ticketPrice) < 0 ||
      !Number.isInteger(Number(event.capacity)) ||
      Number(event.capacity) < bookedSeats
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid price or capacity cannot be below booked seats."
      });
    }

    event.ticketPrice = Number(event.ticketPrice);
    event.capacity = Number(event.capacity);
    event.availableSeats = event.capacity - bookedSeats;

    // Updated events require admin approval again
    event.approvalStatus = "pending";

    await event.save();

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: "Event updated and sent for approval.",
      event
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Failed to update event.",
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// Delete an event owned by the organizer
exports.deleteEvent = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const event = await Event.findOne({
      _id: req.params.id,
      organizer: req.user._id
    });

    if (!event) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Event not found or access denied."
      });
    }

    await event.deleteOne();

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: "Event deleted successfully."
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Failed to delete event.",
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// Get events created by the logged-in organizer
exports.getMyEvents = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const events = await Event.find({
      organizer: req.user._id
    }).sort({ createdAt: -1 });

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      count: events.length,
      events
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Failed to fetch your events.",
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// Mark a notification as read
exports.markAsRead = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const notification = await Notification.findOneAndUpdate(
      {
        _id: req.params.id,
        recipient: req.user._id
      },
      { isRead: true },
      { new: true }
    );

    if (!notification) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Notification not found."
      });
    }

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: "Notification marked as read.",
      notification
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Failed to update notification.",
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// Mark all notifications as read
exports.markAllAsRead = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    await Notification.updateMany(
      { recipient: req.user._id, isRead: false },
      { isRead: true }
    );

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: "All notifications marked as read."
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Failed to update notifications.",
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

const User = require("../models/User");

// Get user profile
exports.getProfile = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const user = await User.findById(req.user._id).select("-password");

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      user
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Failed to fetch profile.",
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

// Update user profile
exports.updateProfile = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { name, interests } = req.body;

    const updates = {};

    if (name !== undefined) {
      if (name.trim().length < 2 || name.trim().length > 60) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: "Name must be between 2 and 60 characters."
        });
      }

      updates.name = name.trim();
    }

    if (interests !== undefined) {
      if (!Array.isArray(interests)) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: "Interests must be an array."
        });
      }

      updates.interests = interests;
    }

    const user = await User.findByIdAndUpdate(
      req.user._id,
      { $set: updates },
      { new: true, runValidators: true }
    ).select("-password");

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: "Profile updated successfully.",
      user
    });
  } catch (error) {
    res.status(500).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Failed to update profile.",
      error: error.message
    });
  }
};

FILE: backend\src\middlewares\auth.middleware.js
==================================================

const jwt = require("jsonwebtoken");
const User = require("../models/User");

exports.protect = async (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    let token;

    if (
      req.headers.authorization &&
      req.headers.authorization.startsWith("Bearer ")
    ) {
      token = req.headers.authorization.split(" ")[1];
    }

    if (!token) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Authentication required. Please log in."
      });
    }

    const decoded = jwt.verify(token, process.env.JWT_SECRET);

    const user = await User.findById(decoded.id);

    if (!user || !user.isActive) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Account not found or inactive."
      });
    }

    req.user = user;
    next();
  } catch (error) {
    return res.status(401).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Invalid or expired authentication token."
    });
  }
};

exports.authorize = (...roles) => {
  return (req, res, next) => {
    if (!roles.includes(req.user.role)) {
      return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message: "You do not have permission to access this resource."
      });
    }

    next();
  };
};



==================================================
FILE: backend\src\middlewares\upload.middleware.js
==================================================

const multer = require("multer");
const path = require("path");
const fs = require("fs");

const uploadDirectory = path.join(__dirname, "../../uploads");

if (!fs.existsSync(uploadDirectory)) {
  fs.mkdirSync(uploadDirectory, { recursive: true });
}

const storage = multer.diskStorage({
  destination: (req, file, cb) => {
    cb(null, uploadDirectory);
  },

  filename: (req, file, cb) => {
    const uniqueName =
      `event-${Date.now()}-${Math.round(Math.random() * 1e9)}` +
      path.extname(file.originalname).toLowerCase();

    cb(null, uniqueName);
  }
});

const fileFilter = (req, file, cb) => {
  const allowedTypes = ["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp"];

  if (allowedTypes.includes(file.mimetype)) {
    cb(null, true);
  } else {
    cb(new Error("Only JPG, PNG and WEBP images are allowed."));
  }
};

const upload = multer({
  storage,
  fileFilter,
  limits: {
    fileSize: 5 * 1024 * 1024
  }
});

module.exports = upload;

const mongoose = require("mongoose");

const bookingSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
  {
    event: {
      type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: "Event",
      required: true
    },
    attendee: {
      type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: "User",
      required: true
    },
    quantity: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: 1,
      default: 1
    },
    totalAmount: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: 0
    },
    bookingStatus: {
      type: String,
      enum: ["confirmed", "cancelled"],
      default: "confirmed"
    },
    paymentStatus: {
      type: String,
      enum: ["pending", "paid", "refunded"],
      default: "pending"
    },
    ticketCode: {
      type: String,
      unique: true,
      sparse: true
    },
    qrCode: {
      type: String,
      default: ""
    }
  },
  { timestamps: true }
);

bookingSchema.index({ attendee: 1, createdAt: -1 });
bookingSchema.index({ event: 1, bookingStatus: 1 });

module.exports = mongoose.model("Booking", bookingSchema);

const mongoose = require("mongoose");

const eventSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
  {
    title: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 120
    },

    description: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      maxlength: 3000
    },

    category: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      enum: [
        "Technology",
        "Music",
        "Sports",
        "Cultural",
        "Workshop",
        "Education",
        "Business",
        "Other"
      ]
    },

    tags: [String],

    poster: {
      type: String,
      default: ""
    },

    organizer: {
      type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: "User",
      required: true
    },

    venue: {
      type: String,
      required: true
    },

    location: {
      type: String,
      required: true
    },

    startDate: {
      type: Date,
      required: true
    },

    endDate: {
      type: Date,
      required: true
    },

    ticketPrice: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: 0,
      default: 0
    },

    capacity: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: 1
    },

    availableSeats: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: 0
    },

    approvalStatus: {
      type: String,
      enum: ["pending", "approved", "rejected"],
      default: "pending"
    },

    status: {
      type: String,
      enum: ["upcoming", "ongoing", "completed", "cancelled"],
      default: "upcoming"
    }
  },
  { timestamps: true }
);

eventSchema.index({ title: "text", description: "text", tags: "text" });
eventSchema.index({ category: 1, startDate: 1 });
eventSchema.index({ organizer: 1, createdAt: -1 });

FILE: backend\src\models\User.js
==================================================

const mongoose = require("mongoose");
const bcrypt = require("bcryptjs");

const userSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
  {
    name: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      minlength: 2,
      maxlength: 60
    },

    email: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      unique: true,
      lowercase: true,
      trim: true
    },

    password: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      minlength: 8,
      select: false
    },

    role: {
      type: String,
      enum: ["user", "organizer", "admin"],
      default: "user"
    },

    organizerStatus: {
      type: String,
      enum: ["not-applied", "pending", "approved", "rejected"],
      default: "not-applied"
    },

    interests: [{
      type: String,
      trim: true
    }],

    avatar: {
      type: String,
      default: ""
    },

    isActive: {
      type: Boolean,
      default: true
    }
  },
  { timestamps: true }
);

// Hash password before saving
userSchema.pre("save", async function () {
  if (!this.isModified("password")) return;
  this.password = await bcrypt.hash(this.password, 12);
});

// Compare entered password with stored hash
userSchema.methods.comparePassword = async function (enteredPassword) {
  return bcrypt.compare(enteredPassword, this.password);
};

module.exports = mongoose.model("User", userSchema);

  "/",
  protect,
  upload.single("poster"),
  createEvent
);

const QRCode = require("qrcode");

const generateQR = async (ticketCode) => {
  try {
    return await QRCode.toDataURL(ticketCode, {
      errorCorrectionLevel: "H",
      width: 300,
      margin: 2
    });
  } catch (error) {
    throw new Error("QR code generation failed.");
  }
};

module.exports = generateQR;


==================================================
FILE: backend\src\validators\auth.validator.js
==================================================

const { body, validationResult } = require("express-validator");

exports.registerValidation = [
  body("name")
    .trim()
    .isLength({ min: 2, max: 60 })
    .withMessage("Name must contain 2 to 60 characters."),

  body("email")
    .trim()
    .isEmail()
    .withMessage("Enter a valid email address.")
    .normalizeEmail(),

  body("password")
    .isLength({ min: 8 })
    .withMessage("Password must contain at least 8 characters.")
];

exports.loginValidation = [
  body("email")
    .trim()
    .isEmail()
    .withMessage("Enter a valid email address.")
    .normalizeEmail(),

  body("password")
    .notEmpty()
    .withMessage("Password is required.")
];

exports.validateRequest = (req, res, next) => {
  const errors = validationResult(req);

  if (!errors.isEmpty()) {
    return res.status(400).json({
      success: false,
      errors: errors.array().map(error => error.msg)
    });
  }

  next();
};

FILE: frontend\README.md
==================================================
# React + Vite

This template provides a minimal setup to get React working in Vite with HMR and some ESLint rules.

Currently, two official plugins are available:

- [@vitejs/plugin-react](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react) uses [Oxc](https://oxc.rs)
- [@vitejs/plugin-react-swc](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react-swc) uses [SWC](https://swc.rs/)

## React Compiler

The React Compiler is not enabled on this template because of its impact on dev & build performances. To add it, see [this documentation](https://react.dev/learn/react-compiler/installation).

## Expanding the ESLint configuration

If you are developing a production application, we recommend using TypeScript with type-aware lint rules enabled. Check out the [TS template](https://github.com/vitejs/vite/tree/main/packages/create-vite/template-react-ts) for information on how to integrate TypeScript and [`typescript-eslint`](https://typescript-eslint.io) in your project.

FILE: frontend\vite.config.js
==================================================
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react'
import { defineConfig } from 'vite'

// https://vite.dev/config/
export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react()],
})

FILE: frontend\src\App.jsx
==================================================

import { Routes, Route, Navigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "./context/AuthContext";
import Login from "./pages/Login";
import Register from "./pages/Register";
import Events from "./pages/Events";
import AdminDashboard from "./pages/AdminDashboard";
import OrganizerDashboard from "./pages/OrganizerDashboard";
import CreateEvent from "./pages/CreateEvent";
import MyEvents from "./pages/MyEvents";
import EditEvent from "./pages/EditEvent";
import OrganizerAttendees from "./pages/OrganizerAttendees";
import OrganizerAnalytics from "./pages/OrganizerAnalytics";
import UserDashboard from "./pages/UserDashboard";
import MyBookings from "./pages/MyBookings";
import MyTickets from "./pages/MyTickets";
import SavedEvents from "./pages/SavedEvents";
import UserProfile from "./pages/UserProfile";
import EventDetails from "./pages/EventDetails";

function getHomePath(user) {
  if (!user) return "/login";
  if (user.role === "admin") return "/admin";
  if (user.role === "organizer") return "/organizer";
  return "/dashboard";
}

function ProtectedRoute({ children, allowedRoles }) {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="loading-screen">Loading EventEase...</div>;
  }

  if (!user) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
  }

  if (allowedRoles && !allowedRoles.includes(user.role)) {
    return <Navigate to={getHomePath(user)} replace />;
  }

  return children;
}

function AdminLanding() {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();

  return (
    <div className="loading-screen">
      <h1>EventEase Admin</h1>
      <p>Welcome, {user?.name || "Administrator"}.</p>
      <p>Your admin account is active.</p>
      <button className="auth-submit" onClick={logout}>
        Sign out
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function App() {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="loading-screen">Loading EventEase...</div>;
  }

  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route
        path="/"
        element={<Navigate to={getHomePath(user)} replace />}
      />

      <Route
        path="/login"
        element={
          user ? <Navigate to={getHomePath(user)} replace /> : <Login />
        }
      />

      <Route
        path="/register"
        element={
          user ? <Navigate to={getHomePath(user)} replace /> : <Register />
        }
      />

      <Route
        path="/events"
        element={
          <ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["user", "organizer"]}>
            <Events />
          </ProtectedRoute>
        }
      />
      <Route
  path="/organizer"
  element={
    <ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["organizer"]}>
      <OrganizerDashboard />
    </ProtectedRoute>
  }
/>
<Route
  path="/organizer/events/new"
  element={
    <ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["organizer"]}>
      <CreateEvent />
    </ProtectedRoute>
  }
/>
<Route
  path="/organizer/events"
  element={
    <ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["organizer"]}>
      <MyEvents />
    </ProtectedRoute>
  }
/>

      <Route
  path="/admin"
  element={
    <ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["admin"]}>
      <AdminDashboard />
    </ProtectedRoute>
  }
/>
<Route
  path="/organizer/events/:id/edit"
  element={
    <ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["organizer"]}>
      <EditEvent />
    </ProtectedRoute>
  }
/>
<Route
  path="/organizer/attendees"
  element={
    <ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["organizer"]}>
      <OrganizerAttendees />
    </ProtectedRoute>
  }
/>
<Route
  path="/organizer/analytics"
  element={
    <ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["organizer"]}>
      <OrganizerAnalytics />
    </ProtectedRoute>
  }
/>
<Route
  path="/dashboard"
  element={
    <ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["user"]}>
      <UserDashboard />
    </ProtectedRoute>
  }
/>
<Route
  path="/user/bookings"
  element={
    <ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["user"]}>
      <MyBookings />
    </ProtectedRoute>
  }
/>
<Route
  path="/user/tickets"
  element={
    <ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["user"]}>
      <MyTickets />
    </ProtectedRoute>
  }
/>
<Route
  path="/user/saved"
  element={
    <ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["user"]}>
      <SavedEvents />
    </ProtectedRoute>
  }
/>
<Route
  path="/user/profile"
  element={
    <ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["user"]}>
      <UserProfile />
    </ProtectedRoute>
  }
/>
<Route
  path="/events/:id"
  element={
    <ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["user"]}>
      <EventDetails />
    </ProtectedRoute>
  }
/>

      <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/" replace />} />
    </Routes>
  );
}

import React from "react";
import ReactDOM from "react-dom/client";

import App from "./App";
import { AuthProvider } from "./context/AuthContext";
import "./App.css";

ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById("root")).render(
  <React.StrictMode>
    <BrowserRouter>
      <AuthProvider>
        <App />
      </AuthProvider>
    </BrowserRouter>
  </React.StrictMode>
);



==================================================
FILE: frontend\src\context\AuthContext.jsx
==================================================

import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import api from "../services/api";

const AuthContext = createContext(null);

export const AuthProvider = ({ children }) => {
  const [user, setUser] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem("eventease_token");

    if (!token) {
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    api.get("/auth/me")
      .then((response) => {
        setUser(response.data.user);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        localStorage.removeItem("eventease_token");
        setUser(null);
      })
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, []);

  const login = async (email, password) => {
    const response = await api.post("/auth/login", { email, password });
    localStorage.setItem("eventease_token", response.data.token);
    setUser(response.data.user);
    return response.data;
  };

  const register = async (name, email, password) => {
    const response = await api.post("/auth/register", {
      name,
      email,
      password
    });

    localStorage.setItem("eventease_token", response.data.token);
    setUser(response.data.user);
    return response.data;
  };

  const logout = async () => {
    try {
      await api.post("/auth/logout");
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Logout request failed:", error.message);
    }

    localStorage.removeItem("eventease_token");
    setUser(null);
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={{ user, loading, login, register, logout }}>
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useAuth = () => useContext(AuthContext);

FILE: frontend\src\pages\AdminDashboard.jsx
==================================================

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
  LayoutDashboard, CalendarDays, Users, Ticket,
  CheckCircle, XCircle, Clock, LogOut, RefreshCw,
  ShieldCheck, Menu, X, TrendingUp
} from "lucide-react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import api from "../services/api";
import "./AdminDashboard.css";

export default function AdminDashboard() {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState("overview");
  const [events, setEvents] = useState([]);
  const [users, setUsers] = useState([]);
  const [organizers, setOrganizers] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [busyId, setBusyId] = useState("");
  const [mobileMenu, setMobileMenu] = useState(false);

  const loadDashboard = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setMessage("");

    try {
      const results = await Promise.allSettled([
        api.get("/admin/users"),
        api.get("/admin/organizer-applications"),
        api.get("/admin/events/pending")
      ]);

      if (results[0].status === "fulfilled") {
        const data = results[0].value.data;
        setUsers(data.users || []);
      }

      if (results[1].status === "fulfilled") {
        const data = results[1].value.data;
        setOrganizers(
          data.organizers || data.applications || data.users || []
        );
      }

      if (results[2].status === "fulfilled") {
        const data = results[2].value.data;
        setEvents(data.events || []);
      }

      if (results.every(result => result.status === "rejected")) {
        setMessage("Could not load dashboard data. Check your backend.");
      }
    } catch {
      setMessage("Something went wrong while loading dashboard data.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadDashboard();
  }, []);

  const reviewEvent = async (id, action) => {
    setBusyId(id);
    try {
      await api.patch(`/admin/events/${id}/review`, { action });
      setEvents(current => current.filter(event => event._id !== id));
      setMessage(`Event ${action}d successfully.`);
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error.response?.data?.message || "Event review failed.");
    } finally {
      setBusyId("");
    }
  };

  const reviewOrganizer = async (id, action) => {
    setBusyId(id);
    try {
      await api.patch(`/admin/organizers/${id}/review`, { action });
      setOrganizers(current => current.filter(item => item._id !== id));
      setMessage(`Organizer application ${action}d successfully.`);
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(
        error.response?.data?.message || "Organizer review failed."
      );
    } finally {
      setBusyId("");
    }
  };

  const navItems = [
    { id: "overview", label: "Overview", icon: LayoutDashboard },
    { id: "events", label: "Event approvals", icon: CalendarDays },
    { id: "organizers", label: "Organizers", icon: ShieldCheck },
    { id: "users", label: "Users", icon: Users }
  ];

  const stats = [
    {
      label: "Total users",
      value: users.length,
      icon: Users
    },
    {
      label: "Pending events",
      value: events.length,
      icon: CalendarDays
    },
    {
      label: "Organizer applications",
      value: organizers.length,
      icon: ShieldCheck
    },
    {
      label: "Total bookings",
      value: "—",
      icon: Ticket
    }
  ];

  const formatDate = date =>
    date ? new Date(date).toLocaleDateString("en-IN") : "—";

  return (
    <div className="admin-layout">
      <aside className={`admin-sidebar ${mobileMenu ? "open" : ""}`}>
        <div className="admin-brand">
          <CalendarDays size={27} />
          <span>Event<span>Ease</span></span>
        </div>

        <div className="admin-side-label">MANAGEMENT</div>

        <nav className="admin-nav">
          {navItems.map(item => {
            const Icon = item.icon;
            return (
              <button
                key={item.id}
                className={activeTab === item.id ? "active" : ""}
                onClick={() => {
                  setActiveTab(item.id);
                  setMobileMenu(false);
                  setMessage("");
                }}
              >
                <Icon size={19} />
                {item.label}
                {item.id === "events" && events.length > 0 && (
                  <span className="nav-count">{events.length}</span>
                )}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </nav>

        <div className="admin-sidebar-bottom">
          <div className="admin-profile">
            <div className="admin-avatar">
              {(user?.name || "A").charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
            </div>
            <div>
              <strong>{user?.name || "Administrator"}</strong>
              <small>Platform Admin</small>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button className="admin-logout" onClick={logout}>
            <LogOut size={18} /> Sign out
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {mobileMenu && (
        <button
          className="admin-overlay"
          onClick={() => setMobileMenu(false)}
          aria-label="Close menu"
        />
      )}

      <main className="admin-main">
        <header className="admin-topbar">
          <button
            className="admin-mobile-toggle"
            onClick={() => setMobileMenu(!mobileMenu)}
          >
            {mobileMenu ? <X /> : <Menu />}
          </button>

          <div>
            <span className="admin-top-eyebrow">EVENTEASE CONTROL CENTRE</span>
            <h2>Administration</h2>
          </div>

          <button
            className="admin-refresh"
            onClick={loadDashboard}
            disabled={loading}
          >
            <RefreshCw size={17} className={loading ? "spinning" : ""} />
            Refresh
          </button>
        </header>

        <section className="admin-content">
          <div className="admin-welcome">
            <div>
              <span className="admin-eyebrow">YOUR PLATFORM AT A GLANCE</span>
              <h1>
                Welcome back, <span>{user?.name?.split(" ")[0] || "Admin"}.</span>
              </h1>
              <p>Manage events, organizers and your EventEase community.</p>
            </div>
            <div className="welcome-icon">
              <ShieldCheck size={54} />
            </div>
          </div>

          {message && (
            <div className="admin-message">
              {message}
              <button onClick={() => setMessage("")}>×</button>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="admin-stats">
            {stats.map(stat => {
              const Icon = stat.icon;
              return (
                <div className="admin-stat-card" key={stat.label}>
                  <div className="stat-icon"><Icon size={21} /></div>
                  <span>{stat.label}</span>
                  <strong>{stat.value}</strong>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          <div className="admin-section-heading">
            <div>
              <span className="admin-eyebrow">WORKSPACE</span>
              <h2>
                {activeTab === "overview" && "Platform overview"}
                {activeTab === "events" && "Pending event approvals"}
                {activeTab === "organizers" && "Organizer applications"}
                {activeTab === "users" && "Registered users"}
              </h2>
            </div>
            <span className="admin-live">
              <span /> Live data
            </span>
          </div>

          {loading ? (
            <div className="admin-empty">
              <RefreshCw className="spinning" size={25} />
              <p>Loading dashboard data...</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              {activeTab === "overview" && (
                <div className="admin-overview-grid">
                  <div className="admin-panel">
                    <div className="panel-heading">
                      <div>
                        <h3>Pending events</h3>
                        <p>Events waiting for your approval</p>
                      </div>
                      <button onClick={() => setActiveTab("events")}>
                        View all
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    {events.length === 0 ? (
                      <div className="admin-small-empty">
                        <CheckCircle size={25} />
                        <p>All caught up! No pending events.</p>
                      </div>
                    ) : events.slice(0, 4).map(event => (
                      <div className="admin-list-row" key={event._id}>
                        <div className="list-icon"><CalendarDays /></div>
                        <div className="list-info">
                          <strong>{event.title}</strong>
                          <small>{event.category} · {event.location}</small>
                        </div>
                        <span className="status-pending">Pending</span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>

                  <div className="admin-panel">
                    <div className="panel-heading">
                      <div>
                        <h3>Organizer applications</h3>
                        <p>People requesting organizer access</p>
                      </div>
                      <button onClick={() => setActiveTab("organizers")}>
                        View all
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    {organizers.length === 0 ? (
                      <div className="admin-small-empty">
                        <CheckCircle size={25} />
                        <p>No pending applications.</p>
                      </div>
                    ) : organizers.slice(0, 4).map(item => (
                      <div className="admin-list-row" key={item._id}>
                        <div className="list-icon"><Users /></div>
                        <div className="list-info">
                          <strong>{item.name}</strong>
                          <small>{item.email}</small>
                        </div>
                        <span className="status-pending">Pending</span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {activeTab === "events" && (
                <div className="admin-panel">
                  {events.length === 0 ? (
                    <div className="admin-empty">
                      <CheckCircle size={35} />
                      <h3>No pending events</h3>
                      <p>New event submissions will appear here.</p>
                    </div>
                  ) : events.map(event => (
                    <div className="approval-card" key={event._id}>
                      <div className="approval-details">
                        <span className="approval-category">{event.category}</span>
                        <h3>{event.title}</h3>
                        <p>{event.description}</p>
                        <div className="approval-meta">
                          <span>{event.location}</span>
                          <span>{formatDate(event.startDate)}</span>
                          <span>₹{event.ticketPrice}</span>
                        </div>
                        <small>
                          Organizer: {event.organizer?.name || "Unknown"}
                        </small>
                      </div>
                      <div className="approval-actions">
                        <button
                          className="approve-btn"
                          disabled={busyId === event._id}
                          onClick={() => reviewEvent(event._id, "approve")}
                        >
                          <CheckCircle size={16} /> Approve
                        </button>
                        <button
                          className="reject-btn"
                          disabled={busyId === event._id}
                          onClick={() => reviewEvent(event._id, "reject")}
                        >
                          <XCircle size={16} /> Reject
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}

              {activeTab === "organizers" && (
                <div className="admin-panel">
                  {organizers.length === 0 ? (
                    <div className="admin-empty">
                      <CheckCircle size={35} />
                      <h3>No pending applications</h3>
                      <p>Organizer applications will appear here.</p>
                    </div>
                  ) : organizers.map(item => (
                    <div className="approval-card" key={item._id}>
                      <div className="approval-details">
                        <span className="approval-category">ORGANIZER REQUEST</span>
                        <h3>{item.name}</h3>
                        <p>{item.email}</p>
                        <small>Application status: Pending</small>
                      </div>
                      <div className="approval-actions">
                        <button
                          className="approve-btn"
                          disabled={busyId === item._id}
                          onClick={() => reviewOrganizer(item._id, "approve")}
                        >
                          <CheckCircle size={16} /> Approve
                        </button>
                        <button
                          className="reject-btn"
                          disabled={busyId === item._id}
                          onClick={() => reviewOrganizer(item._id, "reject")}
                        >
                          <XCircle size={16} /> Reject
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}

              {activeTab === "users" && (
                <div className="admin-panel table-wrap">
                  <table className="admin-table">
                    <thead>
                      <tr>
                        <th>Name</th>
                        <th>Email</th>
                        <th>Role</th>
                        <th>Joined</th>
                      </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                      {users.map(person => (
                        <tr key={person._id}>
                          <td>{person.name}</td>
                          <td>{person.email}</td>
                          <td>
                            <span className="role-pill">{person.role}</span>
                          </td>
                          <td>{formatDate(person.createdAt)}</td>
                        </tr>
                      ))}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                  {users.length === 0 && (
                    <div className="admin-empty">No users found.</div>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </>
          )}

          <footer className="admin-footer">
            <span>EventEase © 2026</span>
            <span>Discover. Experience. Remember.</span>
            <span><TrendingUp size={14} /> Admin workspace</span>
          </footer>
        </section>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend\src\pages\CreateEvent.jsx

import { useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { ArrowLeft, CalendarDays, MapPin, ImagePlus, Upload, Plus, X, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import api from "../services/api";
import "./CreateEvent.css";

const initialForm = {
  title: "",
  description: "",
  category: "Technology",
  venue: "",
  location: "",
  startDate: "",
  endDate: "",
  ticketPrice: 0,
  capacity: 100,
  tags: ""
};

export default function CreateEvent() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user } = useAuth();

  const [form, setForm] = useState(initialForm);
  const [poster, setPoster] = useState(null);
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState("");

  const updateField = (e) => {
    const { name, value } = e.target;
    setForm((previous) => ({ ...previous, [name]: value }));
  };

  const handlePoster = (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    if (!file.type.startsWith("image/")) {
      setError("Please select a valid image.");
      return;
    }

    if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
      setError("Poster must be smaller than 5 MB.");
      return;
    }

    setError("");
    setPoster(file);
    setPreview(URL.createObjectURL(file));
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setSuccess("");

    if (new Date(form.endDate) <= new Date(form.startDate)) {
      setError("End date must be after the start date.");
      return;
    }

    if (Number(form.ticketPrice) < 0 || Number(form.capacity) < 1) {
      setError("Enter a valid ticket price and capacity.");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setLoading(true);

      const data = new FormData();

      Object.entries(form).forEach(([key, value]) => {
        if (key === "tags") {
          data.append(
            "tags",
            JSON.stringify(value.split(",").map((tag) => tag.trim()).filter(Boolean))
          );
        } else {
          data.append(key, value);
        }
      });

      if (poster) data.append("poster", poster);

      const response = await api.post("/events", data);

      setSuccess(response.data.message || "Event submitted successfully.");

      setTimeout(() => navigate("/organizer"), 1200);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message ||
        err.response?.data?.errors?.join(", ") ||
        "Unable to submit event. Please try again."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="create-event-page">
      <header className="create-event-header">
        <button onClick={() => navigate("/organizer")} className="create-back">
          <ArrowLeft size={18} /> Back to dashboard
        </button>
        <div className="create-brand">EventEase <span>ORGANIZER STUDIO</span></div>
      </header>

      <main className="create-event-container">
        <div className="create-event-heading">
          <span>BRING YOUR IDEA TO LIFE</span>
          <h1>Create an event.</h1>
          <p>Share an experience people will remember.</p>
        </div>

        <form className="create-event-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <section className="create-form-section">
            <h2>01. Event information</h2>
            <p>Tell attendees what your event is about.</p>

            <label>Event title *</label>
            <input name="title" value={form.title} onChange={updateField}
              placeholder="e.g. Technology Innovation Summit" required maxLength={100} />

            <label>Event description *</label>
            <textarea name="description" value={form.description} onChange={updateField}
              placeholder="Describe your event, highlights and what attendees can expect..."
              rows={5} required maxLength={2000} />

            <label>Category *</label>
            <select name="category" value={form.category} onChange={updateField}>
              {["Technology", "Music", "Sports", "Cultural", "Workshop", "Education", "Business", "Other"].map((category) => (
                <option key={category} value={category}>{category}</option>
              ))}
            </select>

            <label>Tags</label>
            <input name="tags" value={form.tags} onChange={updateField}
              placeholder="AI, networking, innovation (comma separated)" />
          </section>

          <section className="create-form-section">
            <h2>02. Location and schedule</h2>
            <p>Where and when will it happen?</p>

            <label>Venue name *</label>
            <input name="venue" value={form.venue} onChange={updateField}
              placeholder="Venue or auditorium name" required />

            <label>City / Location *</label>
            <input name="location" value={form.location} onChange={updateField}
              placeholder="e.g. Lucknow" required />

            <div className="create-date-grid">
              <div>
                <label><CalendarDays size={15} /> Start date and time *</label>
                <input type="datetime-local" name="startDate" value={form.startDate}
                  onChange={updateField} required />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label><CalendarDays size={15} /> End date and time *</label>
                <input type="datetime-local" name="endDate" value={form.endDate}
                  onChange={updateField} required />
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="create-form-section">
            <h2>03. Tickets and capacity</h2>
            <p>Set your ticket price and the number of attendees.</p>

            <div className="create-date-grid">
              <div>
                <label>Ticket price (₹) *</label>
                <input type="number" name="ticketPrice" min="0" step="1"
                  value={form.ticketPrice} onChange={updateField} required />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label>Maximum capacity *</label>
                <input type="number" name="capacity" min="1" step="1"
                  value={form.capacity} onChange={updateField} required />
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="create-form-section">
            <h2>04. Event poster</h2>
            <p>Upload an image to represent your event.</p>

            <label className="create-upload-area">
              {preview ? (
                <img src={preview} alt="Event poster preview" />
              ) : (
                <>
                  <ImagePlus size={35} />
                  <strong>Choose your event poster</strong>
                  <span>JPG, PNG or WEBP · Maximum 5 MB</span>
                  <span className="create-upload-button"><Upload size={16} /> Browse files</span>
                </>
              )}
              <input type="file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp"
                onChange={handlePoster} hidden />
            </label>

            {poster && (
              <button type="button" className="create-remove-poster"
                onClick={() => { setPoster(null); setPreview(""); }}>
                <X size={15} /> Remove poster
              </button>
            )}
          </section>

          {error && <div className="create-message error">{error}</div>}
          {success && <div className="create-message success">{success}</div>}

          <div className="create-submit-area">
            <p>Your event will be submitted for administrator approval before publication.</p>
            <button type="submit" disabled={loading}>
              {loading ? <Loader2 className="create-loader" size={18} /> : <Plus size={18} />}
              {loading ? "Submitting..." : "Submit event for approval"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}
