==================================================
FILE: frontend\src\pages\EditEvent.jsx
==================================================

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { ArrowLeft, Save, ImagePlus, X } from "lucide-react";
import api from "../services/api";
import "./EditEvent.css";

const categories = [
  "Technology",
  "Music",
  "Sports",
  "Cultural",
  "Workshop",
  "Education",
  "Business",
  "Other"
];

const initialForm = {
  title: "",
  description: "",
  category: "Technology",
  venue: "",
  location: "",
  startDate: "",
  endDate: "",
  ticketPrice: "0",
  capacity: "",
  tags: ""
};

const formatDateTime = (date) => {
  if (!date) return "";

  const value = new Date(date);
  const local = new Date(
    value.getTime() - value.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000
  );

  return local.toISOString().slice(0, 16);
};

export default function EditEvent() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [form, setForm] = useState(initialForm);
  const [poster, setPoster] = useState(null);
  const [currentPoster, setCurrentPoster] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    const loadEvent = async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true);

        const response = await api.get("/events/my-events");
        const data = response.data;

        const events = Array.isArray(data)
          ? data
          : data.events || data.data?.events || [];

        const event = events.find((item) => item._id === id);

        if (!event) {
          setError("Event not found or you do not have permission to edit it.");
          return;
        }

        setForm({
          title: event.title || "",
          description: event.description || "",
          category: event.category || "Technology",
          venue: event.venue || "",
          location: event.location || "",
          startDate: formatDateTime(event.startDate),
          endDate: formatDateTime(event.endDate),
          ticketPrice: String(event.ticketPrice ?? 0),
          capacity: String(event.capacity ?? ""),
          tags: Array.isArray(event.tags)
            ? event.tags.join(", ")
            : event.tags || ""
        });

        setCurrentPoster(event.poster || "");
      } catch (err) {
        setError(
          err.response?.data?.message || "Unable to load event details."
        );
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };

    loadEvent();
  }, [id]);

  const handleChange = (e) => {
    const { name, value } = e.target;

    setForm((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      [name]: value
    }));
  };

  const handlePosterChange = (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];

    if (!file) return;

    if (!["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp"].includes(file.type)) {
      setError("Please select a JPG, PNG or WEBP image.");
      return;
    }

    if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
      setError("Poster must be smaller than 5 MB.");
      return;
    }

    setError("");
    setPoster(file);
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setSuccess("");

    if (new Date(form.endDate) <= new Date(form.startDate)) {
      setError("End date and time must be after the start date.");
      return;
    }

    if (Number(form.ticketPrice) < 0) {
      setError("Ticket price cannot be negative.");
      return;
    }

    if (!Number.isInteger(Number(form.capacity)) || Number(form.capacity) < 1) {
      setError("Capacity must be a positive whole number.");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setSaving(true);

      const data = new FormData();

      Object.entries(form).forEach(([key, value]) => {
        if (key === "tags") {
          data.append(
            key,
            JSON.stringify(
              value.split(",").map((tag) => tag.trim()).filter(Boolean)
            )
          );
        } else {
          data.append(key, value);
        }
      });

      if (poster) {
        data.append("poster", poster);
      }

      await api.put(`/events/${id}`, data);

      setSuccess("Event updated successfully. It may require admin approval.");

      setTimeout(() => {
        navigate("/organizer/events");
      }, 1200);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message || "Unable to update event."
      );
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="edit-event-loading">
        Loading event details...
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error && !form.title) {
    return (
      <div className="edit-event-loading">
        <p>{error}</p>
        <button onClick={() => navigate("/organizer/events")}>
          Back to My Events
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="edit-event-page">

      <header className="edit-event-header">
        <button
          className="edit-back-button"
          onClick={() => navigate("/organizer/events")}
        >
          <ArrowLeft size={17} />
          Back to My Events
        </button>

        <span>ORGANIZER STUDIO</span>
      </header>

      <main className="edit-event-container">

        <div className="edit-event-heading">
          <span>EVENT MANAGEMENT</span>
          <h1>Edit Event</h1>
          <p>Update your event information and keep your audience informed.</p>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div className="edit-event-alert error">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {success && (
          <div className="edit-event-alert success">
            {success}
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="edit-event-form">

          <section className="edit-form-section">
            <h2>01. Event Information</h2>
            <p>Give your event its identity.</p>

            <div className="edit-form-group">
              <label>Event Title *</label>
              <input
                name="title"
                value={form.title}
                onChange={handleChange}
                required
                maxLength={120}
                placeholder="Enter event title"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="edit-form-group">
              <label>Description *</label>
              <textarea
                name="description"
                value={form.description}
                onChange={handleChange}
                required
                rows={5}
                placeholder="Describe your event..."
              />
            </div>

            <div className="edit-form-grid">
              <div className="edit-form-group">
                <label>Category *</label>
                <select
                  name="category"
                  value={form.category}
                  onChange={handleChange}
                  required
                >
                  {categories.map((category) => (
                    <option key={category} value={category}>
                      {category}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div className="edit-form-group">
                <label>Tags</label>
                <input
                  name="tags"
                  value={form.tags}
                  onChange={handleChange}
                  placeholder="AI, Technology, Innovation"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="edit-form-section">
            <h2>02. Date & Location</h2>
            <p>Where and when will your event happen?</p>

            <div className="edit-form-grid">
              <div className="edit-form-group">
                <label>Venue *</label>
                <input
                  name="venue"
                  value={form.venue}
                  onChange={handleChange}
                  required
                  placeholder="Enter venue name"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="edit-form-group">
                <label>City / Location *</label>
                <input
                  name="location"
                  value={form.location}
                  onChange={handleChange}
                  required
                  placeholder="Enter location"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="edit-form-group">
                <label>Start Date & Time *</label>
                <input
                  type="datetime-local"
                  name="startDate"
                  value={form.startDate}
                  onChange={handleChange}
                  required
                />
              </div>

              <div className="edit-form-group">
                <label>End Date & Time *</label>
                <input
                  type="datetime-local"
                  name="endDate"
                  value={form.endDate}
                  onChange={handleChange}
                  required
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="edit-form-section">
            <h2>03. Tickets & Capacity</h2>
            <p>Set your event's ticket pricing and attendance limit.</p>

            <div className="edit-form-grid">
              <div className="edit-form-group">
                <label>Ticket Price (₹) *</label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  name="ticketPrice"
                  value={form.ticketPrice}
                  onChange={handleChange}
                  min="0"
                  required
                />
              </div>

              <div className="edit-form-group">
                <label>Total Capacity *</label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  name="capacity"
                  value={form.capacity}
                  onChange={handleChange}
                  min="1"
                  step="1"
                  required
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="edit-form-section">
            <h2>04. Event Poster</h2>
            <p>Keep your event artwork fresh.</p>

            {currentPoster && !poster && (
              <div className="edit-current-poster">
                <img
                  src={
                    currentPoster.startsWith("http")
                      ? currentPoster
                      : `http://localhost:5000${currentPoster}`
                  }
                  alt="Current event poster"
                />
                <span>Current poster</span>
              </div>
            )}

            <label className="edit-poster-upload">
              <ImagePlus size={27} />
              <strong>
                {poster ? poster.name : "Choose a replacement poster"}
              </strong>
              <span>JPG, PNG or WEBP · Maximum 5 MB</span>
              <input
                type="file"
                accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp"
                onChange={handlePosterChange}
                hidden
              />
            </label>

            {poster && (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="edit-remove-poster"
                onClick={() => setPoster(null)}
              >
                <X size={15} />
                Remove selected replacement
              </button>
            )}
          </section>

          <div className="edit-event-actions">
            <button
              type="button"
              className="edit-cancel-button"
              onClick={() => navigate("/organizer/events")}
            >
              Cancel
            </button>

            <button
              type="submit"
              className="edit-save-button"
              disabled={saving}
            >
              <Save size={17} />
              {saving ? "Saving Changes..." : "Save Changes"}
            </button>
          </div>

        </form>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate, useParams, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  CalendarDays,
  Clock,
  MapPin,
  Ticket,
  Users,
  Heart,
  Share2,
  ShieldCheck,
  Minus,
  Plus,
  CheckCircle,
  UserRound,
  ArrowRight
} from "lucide-react";
import "./EventDetails.css";

const previewEvents = [
  {
    _id: "6b04780cd82f094bbda3a433",
    title: "Tech Innovation Summit 2026",
    category: "Technology",
    description:
      "Join an exciting gathering of technology enthusiasts, developers and innovators. Explore artificial intelligence, cybersecurity, cloud computing and emerging technologies. Connect with experts and discover ideas shaping the future.",
    date: "December 15, 2026",
    time: "10:00 AM – 5:00 PM",
    location: "Lucknow",
    venue: "SRMU Campus Auditorium",
    price: 199,
    capacity: 100,
    availableSeats: 100,
    organizerName: "EventEase Organizer",
    image:
      "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1540575467063-178a50c2df87?w=1400",
    tags: ["Technology", "AI", "Cybersecurity"]
  },
  {
    _id: "6b04780cd82f094bbda3a434",
    title: "AI & Cybersecurity Summit",
    category: "Technology",
    description:
      "Discover the intersection of artificial intelligence and cybersecurity. Learn about ethical hacking, emerging cyber threats and intelligent security systems.",
    date: "December 20, 2026",
    time: "10:00 AM – 5:00 PM",
    location: "Lucknow",
    venue: "SRMU Campus",
    price: 199,
    capacity: 100,
    availableSeats: 100,
    organizerName: "EventEase Organizer",
    image:
      "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1516321318423-f06f85e504b3?w=1400",
    tags: ["AI", "Cybersecurity", "Innovation"]
  }
];

export default function EventDetails() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();

  const passedEvent = location.state?.event;

  const event = useMemo(() => {
    if (passedEvent) return passedEvent;

    return previewEvents.find((item) => item._id === id) ||
      previewEvents[0];
  }, [id, passedEvent]);

  const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState(1);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(() => {
    try {
      const savedItems = JSON.parse(
        localStorage.getItem("eventease_saved_events") || "[]"
      );
      return savedItems.includes(id);
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  });
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  const price = Number(event.ticketPrice ?? event.price ?? 0);
  const availableSeats = Number(event.availableSeats ?? event.capacity ?? 0);
  const total = price * quantity;

  const formatDate = (date) => {
    if (!date) return "Date to be announced";

    return new Date(date).toLocaleDateString("en-IN", {
      day: "numeric",
      month: "long",
      year: "numeric"
    });
  };

  const startDate = event.startDate
    ? formatDate(event.startDate)
    : event.date;

  const startTime = event.startDate
    ? new Date(event.startDate).toLocaleTimeString("en-IN", {
        hour: "numeric",
        minute: "2-digit"
      })
    : event.time;

  const endTime = event.endDate
    ? new Date(event.endDate).toLocaleTimeString("en-IN", {
        hour: "numeric",
        minute: "2-digit"
      })
    : "";

  const image = event.poster
    ? event.poster.startsWith("http")
      ? event.poster
      : `http://localhost:5000${event.poster}`
    : event.image;

  const organizerName =
    event.organizer?.name ||
    event.organizerName ||
    "EventEase Organizer";

  const handleQuantity = (value) => {
    setQuantity((current) =>
      Math.min(availableSeats, Math.max(1, current + value))
    );
  };

  const handleSave = () => {
    try {
      const savedItems = JSON.parse(
        localStorage.getItem("eventease_saved_events") || "[]"
      );

      let updatedItems;

      if (saved) {
        updatedItems = savedItems.filter((item) => item !== id);
        setMessage("Event removed from your saved list.");
      } else {
        updatedItems = [...new Set([...savedItems, id])];
        setMessage("Event added to your saved list.");
      }

      localStorage.setItem(
        "eventease_saved_events",
        JSON.stringify(updatedItems)
      );

      setSaved(!saved);
    } catch {
      setMessage("Unable to update your saved events.");
    }
  };

  const handleShare = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(window.location.href);
      setMessage("Event link copied to clipboard.");
    } catch {
      setMessage("Unable to copy the event link.");
    }
  };

  const handleBooking = () => {
    setMessage(
      "Booking will be enabled when the booking API is connected."
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="event-details-page">

      {/* Navigation */}
      <header className="event-details-nav">
        <div
          className="event-details-brand"
          onClick={() => navigate("/events")}
        >
          <span className="event-details-brand-icon">E</span>
          <span>EventEase</span>
        </div>

        <button
          className="event-back-button"
          onClick={() => navigate("/events")}
        >
          <ArrowLeft size={17} />
          Back to events
        </button>
      </header>

      <main className="event-details-container">

        {/* Breadcrumb */}
        <div className="event-breadcrumb">
          <span onClick={() => navigate("/events")}>Explore Events</span>
          <ArrowRight size={13} />
          <span>{event.category}</span>
          <ArrowRight size={13} />
          <strong>Event Details</strong>
        </div>

        {/* Hero */}
        <section className="event-details-hero">
          <img
            src={image}
            alt={event.title}
            onError={(e) => {
              e.currentTarget.src =
                "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1540575467063-178a50c2df87?w=1400";
            }}
          />

          <div className="event-details-hero-overlay" />

          <div className="event-details-hero-content">
            <span className="event-category-badge">
              {event.category}
            </span>

            <h1>{event.title}</h1>

            <div className="event-hero-meta">
              <span><CalendarDays size={16} /> {startDate}</span>
              <span><MapPin size={16} /> {event.location || event.venue}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        {message && (
          <div className="event-details-message">
            <CheckCircle size={17} />
            {message}
            <button onClick={() => setMessage("")}>×</button>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="event-details-layout">

          {/* Main event information */}
          <section className="event-details-information">

            <div className="event-info-heading">
              <div>
                <span className="event-section-eyebrow">
                  THE EXPERIENCE
                </span>
                <h2>About this event</h2>
              </div>

              <div className="event-detail-actions">
                <button
                  className={saved ? "event-action-button saved" : "event-action-button"}
                  onClick={handleSave}
                  title="Save event"
                >
                  <Heart size={18} fill={saved ? "currentColor" : "none"} />
                </button>

                <button
                  className="event-action-button"
                  onClick={handleShare}
                  title="Share event"
                >
                  <Share2 size={18} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <p className="event-description">
              {event.description || "More information about this event will be available soon."}
            </p>

            {/* Event facts */}
            <div className="event-facts-grid">

              <div className="event-fact">
                <div className="event-fact-icon">
                  <CalendarDays size={21} />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>DATE</span>
                  <strong>{startDate}</strong>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="event-fact">
                <div className="event-fact-icon">
                  <Clock size={21} />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>TIME</span>
                  <strong>
                    {startTime}
                    {endTime ? ` – ${endTime}` : ""}
                  </strong>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="event-fact">
                <div className="event-fact-icon">
                  <MapPin size={21} />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>VENUE</span>
                  <strong>{event.venue || event.location || "Venue to be announced"}</strong>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="event-fact">
                <div className="event-fact-icon">
                  <Users size={21} />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>AVAILABLE SEATS</span>
                  <strong>{availableSeats} seats</strong>
                </div>
              </div>

            </div>

            {/* Tags */}
            {event.tags?.length > 0 && (
              <div className="event-tags-section">
                <h3>Event Highlights</h3>
                <div className="event-tags">
                  {event.tags.map((tag, index) => (
                    <span key={`${tag}-${index}`}>{tag}</span>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Organizer */}
            <div className="event-organizer-card">
              <div className="event-organizer-avatar">
                <UserRound size={25} />
              </div>

              <div>
                <span>ORGANIZED BY</span>
                <h3>{organizerName}</h3>
                <p>Event organizer</p>
              </div>

              <ShieldCheck size={22} className="event-organizer-verified" />
            </div>

          </section>

          {/* Booking card */}
          <aside className="event-booking-card">

            <div className="event-booking-heading">
              <span className="event-section-eyebrow">
                RESERVE YOUR SPOT
              </span>
              <h2>Book your tickets</h2>
              <p>Be part of an unforgettable experience.</p>
            </div>

            <div className="event-ticket-price">
              <div>
                <span>Ticket price</span>
                <strong>₹{price.toLocaleString("en-IN")}</strong>
              </div>
              <span className="event-ticket-unit">/ person</span>
            </div>

            <div className="event-quantity-section">
              <div>
                <strong>Number of tickets</strong>
                <span>Select your quantity</span>
              </div>

              <div className="event-quantity-control">
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleQuantity(-1)}
                  disabled={quantity <= 1}
                  aria-label="Decrease ticket quantity"
                >
                  <Minus size={15} />
                </button>

                <strong>{quantity}</strong>

                <button
                  onClick={() => handleQuantity(1)}
                  disabled={quantity >= availableSeats || availableSeats <= 0}
                  aria-label="Increase ticket quantity"
                >
                  <Plus size={15} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="event-booking-total">
              <span>Total amount</span>
              <strong>₹{total.toLocaleString("en-IN")}</strong>
            </div>

            <button
              className="event-book-now"
              onClick={handleBooking}
              disabled={availableSeats <= 0}
            >
              <Ticket size={18} />
              {availableSeats > 0 ? "Book Now" : "Sold Out"}
              {availableSeats > 0 && <ArrowRight size={17} />}
            </button>

            <div className="event-booking-security">
              <ShieldCheck size={16} />
              Secure booking experience
            </div>

            <div className="event-booking-note">
              <p>
                <CheckCircle size={14} />
                Instant booking confirmation
              </p>
              <p>
                <CheckCircle size={14} />
                Digital ticket access
              </p>
              <p>
                <CheckCircle size={14} />
                QR-based event entry
              </p>
            </div>

          </aside>

        </div>

        {/* Bottom section */}
        <section className="event-bottom-cta">
          <div>
            <span>DON'T MISS OUT</span>
            <h2>Make this experience yours.</h2>
            <p>Discover more events and create unforgettable memories.</p>
          </div>

          <button onClick={() => navigate("/events")}>
            Explore more events <ArrowRight size={17} />
          </button>
        </section>

        <footer className="event-details-footer">
          <span>EVENTEASE © 2026</span>
          <span>Every moment deserves an experience.</span>
        </footer>

      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend\src\pages\Events.jsx

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
  CalendarDays,
  Search,
  MapPin,
  ArrowRight,
  LogOut,
  Ticket,
  Sparkles,
  Music2,
  Clock3
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import api from "../services/api";

const categories = [
  "All Events",
  "Technology",
  "Music",
  "Sports",
  "Cultural",
  "Workshop",
  "Education",
  "Business"
];

export default function Events() {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [events, setEvents] = useState([]);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("All Events");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(fetchEvents, 300);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [search, category]);

  const fetchEvents = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const response = await api.get("/events", {
        params: {
          search: search.trim() || undefined,
          category: category === "All Events" ? undefined : category
        }
      });

      setEvents(response.data.events || []);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message || "Unable to load events."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await logout();
    navigate("/login");
  };

  const formatDate = (date) =>
    new Date(date).toLocaleDateString("en-IN", {
      day: "numeric",
      month: "short",
      year: "numeric"
    });

  return (
    <div className="cinema-page">

      {/* NAVIGATION */}
      <header className="cinema-navbar">
        <div
          className="cinema-logo"
          onClick={() => navigate("/events")}
        >
          <CalendarDays size={25} />
          <span>EventEase</span>
        </div>

        <nav>
  <a href="#discover">Discover</a>
  <a href="#featured">Experiences</a>

  <button
    onClick={() =>
      navigate(user?.role === "organizer" ? "/organizer" : "/dashboard")
    }
  >
    Dashboard
  </button>

  {user?.role === "user" && (
    <button onClick={() => navigate("/user/bookings")}>
      My Bookings
    </button>
  )}
</nav>

        <div className="cinema-account">
          <span>{user?.name?.split(" ")[0] || "Guest"}</span>
          <button onClick={handleLogout} title="Logout">
            <LogOut size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* CINEMATIC HERO */}
      <section className="cinema-hero">
        <div className="cinema-overlay" />

        <div className="cinema-hero-content">
          <div className="cinema-eyebrow">
            <Sparkles size={15} />
            WHERE EXPERIENCES COME ALIVE
          </div>

          <h1>
            Your gateway to
            <span> unforgettable events.</span>
          </h1>

          <p>
            Discover extraordinary concerts, festivals,
            workshops and experiences worth remembering.
          </p>

          <button
            className="cinema-cta"
            onClick={() =>
              document.getElementById("discover")?.scrollIntoView({
                behavior: "smooth"
              })
            }
          >
            Discover experiences
            <ArrowRight size={17} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* FLOATING GLASS SEARCH PANEL */}
        <div className="glass-search-panel">
          <div className="glass-panel-heading">
            <div>
              <span>YOUR NEXT EXPERIENCE STARTS HERE</span>
              <h2>Find your event</h2>
            </div>
            <Ticket size={30} />
          </div>

          <div className="cinema-search-fields">
            <label className="cinema-search-input">
              <Search size={18} />
              <input
                value={search}
                onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === "Enter") fetchEvents();
                }}
                placeholder="Search concerts, events..."
              />
            </label>

            <label className="cinema-select">
              <select
                value={category}
                onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value)}
              >
                {categories.map((item) => (
                  <option key={item} value={item}>
                    {item}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </label>

            <button onClick={fetchEvents}>
              Find Events <ArrowRight size={17} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* MAIN CONTENT */}
      <main className="cinema-main">

        {/* INTRODUCTION */}
        <section className="cinema-intro" id="featured">
          <div>
            <span className="cinema-eyebrow dark">
              <Sparkles size={13} />
              MADE FOR THE MOMENTS THAT MATTER
            </span>

            <h2>
              More than events.
              <span> Memories in the making.</span>
            </h2>
          </div>

          <p>
            From unforgettable live performances to
            inspiring new experiences, there's always
            something waiting to be discovered.
          </p>
        </section>

        {/* DISCOVERY */}
        <section className="cinema-discovery" id="discover">
          <div className="cinema-section-heading">
            <div>
              <span className="section-label">EXPLORE THE EXTRAORDINARY</span>
              <h2>Discover Events</h2>
              <p>Find your next reason to get excited.</p>
            </div>

            <span className="event-count">
              {events.length} events
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="cinema-categories">
            {categories.map((item) => (
              <button
                key={item}
                className={category === item ? "active" : ""}
                onClick={() => setCategory(item)}
              >
                {item}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          {loading ? (
            <div className="cinema-state">
              <div className="cinema-spinner" />
              <p>Discovering experiences...</p>
            </div>
          ) : error ? (
            <div className="cinema-state">
              <p>{error}</p>
              <button onClick={fetchEvents}>Try again</button>
            </div>
          ) : events.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="cinema-empty">
              <div className="cinema-empty-icon">
                <CalendarDays size={32} />
              </div>
              <h3>Something exciting is on its way.</h3>
              <p>
                No approved events match your search right now.
                Check back soon for new experiences.
              </p>
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setSearch("");
                  setCategory("All Events");
                }}
              >
                Explore all events
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="cinema-event-grid">
              {events.map((event) => (
                <article
                  className="cinema-event-card"
                  key={event._id}
                >
                  <div className="cinema-event-image">
                    {event.poster ? (
                      <img
  src={
    event.poster?.startsWith("http")
      ? event.poster
      : `http://localhost:5000${event.poster}`
  }
  alt={event.title}
/>
                    ) : (
                      <div className="cinema-poster-placeholder">
                        <Music2 size={40} />
                      </div>
                    )}

                    <span>{event.category}</span>
                  </div>

                  <div className="cinema-event-info">
                    <h3>{event.title}</h3>
                    <p>{event.description}</p>

                    <div className="cinema-event-detail">
                      <span>
                        <CalendarDays size={15} />
                        {formatDate(event.startDate)}
                      </span>

                      <span>
                        <MapPin size={15} />
                        {event.location}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="cinema-event-bottom">
                      <div>
                        <small>Starting from</small>
                        <strong>
                          {Number(event.ticketPrice) === 0
                            ? "Free"
                            : `₹${event.ticketPrice}`}
                        </strong>
                      </div>

                      <button
                        onClick={() =>
                          navigate(`/events/${event._id}`)
                        }
                      >
                        Explore <ArrowRight size={15} />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </article>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
      </main>

      {/* FOOTER */}
      <footer className="cinema-footer">
        <div className="cinema-logo">
          <CalendarDays size={22} />
          <span>EventEase</span>
        </div>

        <p>Discover. Experience. Remember.</p>

        <span>© 2026 EventEase</span>
      </footer>

    </div>
  );
}



==================================================
FILE: frontend\src\pages\Login.jsx
==================================================

import { useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { CalendarDays, Mail, Lock, Eye, EyeOff } from "lucide-react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";

export default function Login() {
  const { login } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [form, setForm] = useState({ email: "", password: "" });
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      await login(form.email, form.password);
      navigate("/events");
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.message || "Login failed. Please try again.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="auth-page">
      <div className="auth-card">
        <Link to="/" className="auth-brand">
          <CalendarDays size={30} />
          <span>Event<span className="brand-accent">Ease</span></span>
        </Link>

        <h1>Welcome back!</h1>
        <p className="auth-subtitle">Sign in to discover amazing events.</p>

        {error && <div className="error-message">{error}</div>}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <label>Email address</label>
          <div className="input-wrapper">
            <Mail size={19} />
            <input
              type="email"
              placeholder="you@example.com"
              value={form.email}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, email: e.target.value })}
              required
            />
          </div>

          <label>Password</label>
          <div className="input-wrapper">
            <Lock size={19} />
            <input
              type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
              placeholder="Enter your password"
              value={form.password}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, password: e.target.value })}
              required
            />
            <button
              type="button"
              className="password-toggle"
              onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
            >
              {showPassword ? <EyeOff size={19} /> : <Eye size={19} />}
            </button>
          </div>

          <button className="auth-submit" disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? "Signing in..." : "Sign In"}
          </button>
        </form>

        <p className="auth-footer">
          Don't have an account? <Link to="/register">Create account</Link>
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend\src\pages\MyBookings.jsx

import React, { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  Compass,
  Ticket,
  CalendarDays,
  Heart,
  UserRound,
  LogOut,
  Menu,
  X,
  Search,
  MapPin,
  Clock,
  ChevronRight,
  ArrowUpRight,
  QrCode,
  Filter,
  Eye,
  XCircle
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import "./MyBookings.css";

const previewBookings = [
  {
    id: "EVT-2026-001",
    title: "Tech Innovation Summit 2026",
    category: "Technology",
    date: "15 December 2026",
    time: "10:00 AM – 5:00 PM",
    venue: "SRMU Campus Auditorium",
    location: "Lucknow",
    bookedOn: "20 November 2026",
    quantity: 1,
    amount: 199,
    payment: "Paid",
    status: "Confirmed"
  },
  {
    id: "EVT-2026-002",
    title: "AI & Cybersecurity Summit",
    category: "Technology",
    date: "20 December 2026",
    time: "10:00 AM – 5:00 PM",
    venue: "SRMU Campus",
    location: "Lucknow",
    bookedOn: "22 November 2026",
    quantity: 1,
    amount: 199,
    payment: "Pending",
    status: "Pending"
  },
  {
    id: "EVT-2026-003",
    title: "Creative Design Workshop",
    category: "Workshop",
    date: "12 October 2026",
    time: "11:00 AM – 2:00 PM",
    venue: "Innovation Lab",
    location: "Lucknow",
    bookedOn: "01 October 2026",
    quantity: 2,
    amount: 398,
    payment: "Paid",
    status: "Completed"
  }
];

function MyBookings() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();

  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState("");
  const [activeFilter, setActiveFilter] = useState("All");
  const [selectedBooking, setSelectedBooking] = useState(null);

  const firstName = user?.name?.split(" ")[0] || "there";

  const filteredBookings = useMemo(() => {
    return previewBookings.filter((booking) => {
      const matchesSearch =
        booking.title.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
        booking.id.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
        booking.location.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase());

      const matchesFilter =
        activeFilter === "All" || booking.status === activeFilter;

      return matchesSearch && matchesFilter;
    });
  }, [searchTerm, activeFilter]);

  const confirmedCount = previewBookings.filter(
    (booking) => booking.status === "Confirmed"
  ).length;

  const pendingCount = previewBookings.filter(
    (booking) => booking.status === "Pending"
  ).length;

  const completedCount = previewBookings.filter(
    (booking) => booking.status === "Completed"
  ).length;

  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout();
    navigate("/login");
  };

  return (
    <div className="mybookings-layout">
      <aside className={`mybookings-sidebar ${menuOpen ? "open" : ""}`}>
        <div className="mybookings-brand">
          <div className="mybookings-brand-icon">E</div>
          <div>
            <h2>EventEase</h2>
            <span>YOUR EVENT WORLD</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mybookings-sidebar-label">MENU</div>

        <nav className="mybookings-navigation">
          <button onClick={() => navigate("/dashboard")}>
            <LayoutDashboard size={19} />
            Dashboard
          </button>

          <button onClick={() => navigate("/events")}>
            <Compass size={19} />
            Explore Events
          </button>

          <button className="active">
            <Ticket size={19} />
            My Bookings
          </button>

          <button onClick={() => navigate("/user/tickets")}>
            <QrCode size={19} />
            My Tickets
          </button>

          <button onClick={() => navigate("/user/saved")}>
            <Heart size={19} />
            Saved Events
          </button>

          <button onClick={() => navigate("/user/profile")}>
            <UserRound size={19} />
            My Profile
          </button>
        </nav>

        <div className="mybookings-sidebar-bottom">
          <div className="mybookings-promo">
            <CalendarDays size={20} />
            <h4>More to experience</h4>
            <p>Discover events and make new memories.</p>
            <button onClick={() => navigate("/events")}>
              Explore events <ArrowUpRight size={14} />
            </button>
          </div>

          <button className="mybookings-logout" onClick={handleLogout}>
            <LogOut size={18} />
            Sign Out
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {menuOpen && (
        <div
          className="mybookings-overlay"
          onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
        />
      )}

      <main className="mybookings-main">
        <header className="mybookings-topbar">
          <div className="mybookings-topbar-left">
            <button
              className="mybookings-menu-toggle"
              onClick={() => setMenuOpen(!menuOpen)}
            >
              {menuOpen ? <X size={22} /> : <Menu size={22} />}
            </button>
            <span>My Space</span>
            <ChevronRight size={15} />
            <strong>My Bookings</strong>
          </div>

          <div className="mybookings-topbar-right">
            <span>EVENT ENTHUSIAST</span>
            <div className="mybookings-avatar">
              {firstName.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
            </div>
          </div>
        </header>

        <div className="mybookings-content">
          <section className="mybookings-heading">
            <div>
              <span className="mybookings-eyebrow">YOUR RESERVATIONS</span>
              <h1>My Bookings</h1>
              <p>
                All your event reservations, together in one place.
              </p>
            </div>

            <button
              className="mybookings-explore-button"
              onClick={() => navigate("/events")}
            >
              <Compass size={17} />
              Discover Events
            </button>
          </section>

          <div className="mybookings-preview-notice">
            <Filter size={17} />
            <span>
              Preview records are displayed here. Your actual bookings will
              appear after API integration.
            </span>
          </div>

          <section className="mybookings-summary">
            <div className="mybookings-summary-card">
              <div className="mybookings-summary-icon total">
                <Ticket size={20} />
              </div>
              <div>
                <span>Total Bookings</span>
                <h2>{previewBookings.length}</h2>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mybookings-summary-card">
              <div className="mybookings-summary-icon confirmed">
                <CalendarDays size={20} />
              </div>
              <div>
                <span>Confirmed</span>
                <h2>{confirmedCount}</h2>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mybookings-summary-card">
              <div className="mybookings-summary-icon pending">
                <Clock size={20} />
              </div>
              <div>
                <span>Pending</span>
                <h2>{pendingCount}</h2>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mybookings-summary-card">
              <div className="mybookings-summary-icon completed">
                <XCircle size={20} />
              </div>
              <div>
                <span>Completed</span>
                <h2>{completedCount}</h2>
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="mybookings-list-section">
            <div className="mybookings-list-heading">
              <div>
                <h2>Booking History</h2>
                <p>Search and manage your event reservations.</p>
              </div>
              <span className="mybookings-record-count">
                {filteredBookings.length} records
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="mybookings-toolbar">
              <div className="mybookings-search">
                <Search size={17} />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="Search by event, booking ID or location..."
                  value={searchTerm}
                  onChange={(event) => setSearchTerm(event.target.value)}
                />
              </div>

              <div className="mybookings-filters">
                {["All", "Confirmed", "Pending", "Completed"].map((filter) => (
                  <button
                    key={filter}
                    className={activeFilter === filter ? "active" : ""}
                    onClick={() => setActiveFilter(filter)}
                  >
                    {filter}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mybookings-records">
              {filteredBookings.length > 0 ? (
                filteredBookings.map((booking) => (
                  <article className="mybookings-record" key={booking.id}>
                    <div className="mybookings-record-icon">
                      <CalendarDays size={22} />
                    </div>

                    <div className="mybookings-record-info">
                      <div className="mybookings-record-title">
                        <h3>{booking.title}</h3>
                        <span
                          className={`mybookings-status ${booking.status.toLowerCase()}`}
                        >
                          {booking.status}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <span className="mybookings-record-category">
                        {booking.category}
                      </span>

                      <div className="mybookings-record-meta">
                        <span>
                          <CalendarDays size={14} />
                          {booking.date}
                        </span>
                        <span>
                          <Clock size={14} />
                          {booking.time}
                        </span>
                        <span>
                          <MapPin size={14} />
                          {booking.venue}, {booking.location}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <div className="mybookings-record-id">
                        Booking ID: {booking.id}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mybookings-record-payment">
                      <strong>₹{booking.amount}</strong>
                      <span>{booking.payment}</span>
                      <small>{booking.quantity} ticket(s)</small>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mybookings-record-actions">
                      <button
                        className="mybookings-details-button"
                        onClick={() => setSelectedBooking(booking)}
                      >
                        <Eye size={15} />
                        Details
                      </button>

                      {booking.status === "Confirmed" && (
                        <button
                          className="mybookings-ticket-button"
                          onClick={() => navigate("/user/tickets")}
                        >
                          <QrCode size={15} />
                          Ticket
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </article>
                ))
              ) : (
                <div className="mybookings-empty">
                  <div>
                    <Search size={27} />
                  </div>
                  <h3>No bookings found</h3>
                  <p>
                    Try another search term or select a different status.
                  </p>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => {
                      setSearchTerm("");
                      setActiveFilter("All");
                    }}
                  >
                    Clear filters
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </section>

          <footer className="mybookings-footer">
            <span>© 2026 EventEase. Made for experiences.</span>
            <span>Your next story starts here.</span>
          </footer>
        </div>
      </main>

      {selectedBooking && (
        <div
          className="mybookings-modal-backdrop"
          onClick={() => setSelectedBooking(null)}
        >
          <div
            className="mybookings-modal"
            onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <div className="mybookings-modal-heading">
              <div>
                <span>RESERVATION DETAILS</span>
                <h2>Booking Information</h2>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => setSelectedBooking(null)}>
                <X size={20} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <h3>{selectedBooking.title}</h3>
            <span className="mybookings-modal-category">
              {selectedBooking.category}
            </span>

            <div className="mybookings-modal-details">
              <div>
                <span>Booking ID</span>
                <strong>{selectedBooking.id}</strong>
              </div>
              <div>
                <span>Event Date</span>
                <strong>{selectedBooking.date}</strong>
              </div>
              <div>
                <span>Time</span>
                <strong>{selectedBooking.time}</strong>
              </div>
              <div>
                <span>Venue</span>
                <strong>{selectedBooking.venue}</strong>
              </div>
              <div>
                <span>Booked On</span>
                <strong>{selectedBooking.bookedOn}</strong>
              </div>
              <div>
                <span>Tickets</span>
                <strong>{selectedBooking.quantity}</strong>
              </div>
              <div>
                <span>Payment Status</span>
                <strong>{selectedBooking.payment}</strong>
              </div>
              <div>
                <span>Booking Status</span>
                <strong>{selectedBooking.status}</strong>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mybookings-modal-total">
              <span>Total Amount</span>
              <strong>₹{selectedBooking.amount}</strong>
            </div>

            <button
              className="mybookings-modal-close"
              onClick={() => setSelectedBooking(null)}
            >
              Close Details
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default MyBookings;

FILE: frontend\src\pages\MyEvents.jsx

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  Search, Plus, CalendarDays, MapPin, Pencil,
  Trash2, RefreshCw, Eye, Ticket
} from "lucide-react";
import api from "../services/api";
import "./MyEvents.css";

export default function MyEvents() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [events, setEvents] = useState([]);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("All");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const fetchEvents = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      const response = await api.get("/events/my-events");
      const data = response.data;

      setEvents(
        Array.isArray(data) ? data :
        data.events || data.data?.events || []
      );
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.message || "Unable to load your events.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchEvents();
  }, []);

  const filteredEvents = useMemo(() => {
    return events.filter((event) => {
      const matchesSearch =
        event.title?.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
        event.location?.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
        event.venue?.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase());

      const eventStatus = event.approvalStatus || "pending";

      const matchesStatus =
        status === "All" ||
        eventStatus.toLowerCase() === status.toLowerCase();

      return matchesSearch && matchesStatus;
    });
  }, [events, search, status]);

  const handleDelete = async (event) => {
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      `Are you sure you want to delete "${event.title}"?`
    );

    if (!confirmed) return;

    try {
      await api.delete(`/events/${event._id}`);
      setEvents((previous) =>
        previous.filter((item) => item._id !== event._id)
      );
    } catch (err) {
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || "Could not delete this event.");
    }
  };

  const formatDate = (date) => {
    if (!date) return "Date not set";
    return new Date(date).toLocaleDateString("en-IN", {
      day: "numeric",
      month: "short",
      year: "numeric"
    });
  };

  const getStatus = (event) => {
    const value = event.approvalStatus || "pending";
    return value.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + value.slice(1);
  };

  return (
    <div className="my-events-page">
      <header className="my-events-header">
        <div>
          <span className="my-events-eyebrow">EVENT MANAGEMENT</span>
          <h1>My Events</h1>
          <p>Manage, track and grow your experiences.</p>
        </div>

        <div className="my-events-header-actions">
          <button className="me-refresh-btn" onClick={fetchEvents}>
            <RefreshCw size={16} /> Refresh
          </button>
          <button
            className="me-create-btn"
            onClick={() => navigate("/organizer/events/new")}
          >
            <Plus size={17} /> Create Event
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      <main className="my-events-content">
        <section className="me-summary">
          <div>
            <span>Total Events</span>
            <strong>{events.length}</strong>
          </div>
          <div>
            <span>Approved</span>
            <strong>
              {events.filter(e => e.approvalStatus === "approved").length}
            </strong>
          </div>
          <div>
            <span>Pending Review</span>
            <strong>
              {events.filter(e => e.approvalStatus === "pending").length}
            </strong>
          </div>
          <div>
            <span>Rejected</span>
            <strong>
              {events.filter(e => e.approvalStatus === "rejected").length}
            </strong>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="me-toolbar">
          <div className="me-search">
            <Search size={18} />
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Search events by title, venue or location..."
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>

          <select
            value={status}
            onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)}
            aria-label="Filter by approval status"
          >
            <option value="All">All statuses</option>
            <option value="approved">Approved</option>
            <option value="pending">Pending</option>
            <option value="rejected">Rejected</option>
          </select>
        </section>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="me-message">Loading your events...</div>
        ) : error ? (
          <div className="me-message me-error">{error}</div>
        ) : filteredEvents.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="me-empty">
            <CalendarDays size={42} />
            <h2>No events found</h2>
            <p>
              {events.length === 0
                ? "You haven't created any events yet."
                : "Try changing your search or filter."}
            </p>
            {events.length === 0 && (
              <button
                className="me-create-btn"
                onClick={() => navigate("/organizer/events/new")}
              >
                <Plus size={17} /> Create your first event
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <section className="me-event-list">
            {filteredEvents.map((event) => (
              <article className="me-event-card" key={event._id}>
                <div className="me-event-poster">
                  {event.poster ? (
                    <img
                      src={
                        event.poster.startsWith("http")
                          ? event.poster
                          : `http://localhost:5000${event.poster}`
                      }
                      alt={event.title}
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <CalendarDays size={30} />
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="me-event-info">
                  <span className="me-category">
                    {event.category || "EVENT"}
                  </span>
                  <h3>{event.title}</h3>

                  <div className="me-event-meta">
                    <span>
                      <CalendarDays size={14} />
                      {formatDate(event.startDate)}
                    </span>
                    <span>
                      <MapPin size={14} />
                      {event.location || event.venue}
                    </span>
                    <span>
                      <Ticket size={14} />
                      ₹{event.ticketPrice ?? 0}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="me-event-actions">
                  <span
                    className={`me-status ${getStatus(event).toLowerCase()}`}
                  >
                    {getStatus(event)}
                  </span>

                  <button
                    title="Edit event"
                    onClick={() =>
                      navigate(`/organizer/events/${event._id}/edit`)
                    }
                  >
                    <Pencil size={17} />
                  </button>

                  <button
                    title="View event"
                    onClick={() => navigate(`/events/${event._id}`)}
                  >
                    <Eye size={17} />
                  </button>

                  <button
                    className="me-delete-btn"
                    title="Delete event"
                    onClick={() => handleDelete(event)}
                  >
                    <Trash2 size={17} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </article>
            ))}
          </section>
        )}
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  Compass,
  Ticket,
  CalendarDays,
  Heart,
  UserRound,
  LogOut,
  Menu,
  X,
  MapPin,
  Clock,
  QrCode,
  Download,
  Printer,
  CheckCircle2,
  ShieldCheck,
  ArrowUpRight
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import "./MyTickets.css";

const previewTickets = [
  {
    id: "EE-2026-001-AX72",
    bookingId: "EVT-2026-001",
    title: "Tech Innovation Summit 2026",
    category: "Technology",
    date: "15 December 2026",
    time: "10:00 AM – 5:00 PM",
    venue: "SRMU Campus Auditorium",
    location: "Lucknow",
    attendee: "Event Attendee",
    ticketType: "General Admission",
    quantity: 1,
    price: 199,
    status: "Confirmed"
  }
];

function MyTickets() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedTicket, setSelectedTicket] = useState(previewTickets[0]);

  const firstName = user?.name?.split(" ")[0] || "there";

  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout();
    navigate("/login");
  };

  const handlePrint = () => {
    window.print();
  };

  return (
    <div className="mytickets-layout">
      <aside className={`mytickets-sidebar ${menuOpen ? "open" : ""}`}>
        <div className="mytickets-brand">
          <div className="mytickets-brand-icon">E</div>
          <div>
            <h2>EventEase</h2>
            <span>YOUR EVENT WORLD</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mytickets-sidebar-label">MENU</div>

        <nav className="mytickets-navigation">
          <button onClick={() => navigate("/dashboard")}>
            <LayoutDashboard size={19} />
            Dashboard
          </button>

          <button onClick={() => navigate("/events")}>
            <Compass size={19} />
            Explore Events
          </button>

          <button onClick={() => navigate("/user/bookings")}>
            <Ticket size={19} />
            My Bookings
          </button>

          <button className="active">
            <QrCode size={19} />
            My Tickets
          </button>

          <button onClick={() => navigate("/user/saved")}>
            <Heart size={19} />
            Saved Events
          </button>

          <button onClick={() => navigate("/user/profile")}>
            <UserRound size={19} />
            My Profile
          </button>
        </nav>

        <div className="mytickets-sidebar-bottom">
          <div className="mytickets-promo">
            <ShieldCheck size={20} />
            <h4>Your ticket, your entry.</h4>
            <p>Keep your digital ticket ready when you arrive.</p>
          </div>

          <button className="mytickets-logout" onClick={handleLogout}>
            <LogOut size={18} />
            Sign Out
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {menuOpen && (
        <div
          className="mytickets-overlay"
          onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
        />
      )}

      <main className="mytickets-main">
        <header className="mytickets-topbar">
          <div className="mytickets-topbar-left">
            <button
              className="mytickets-menu-toggle"
              onClick={() => setMenuOpen(!menuOpen)}
            >
              {menuOpen ? <X size={22} /> : <Menu size={22} />}
            </button>
            <span>My Space</span>
            <span>/</span>
            <strong>My Tickets</strong>
          </div>

          <div className="mytickets-topbar-right">
            <span>EVENT ENTHUSIAST</span>
            <div className="mytickets-avatar">
              {firstName.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
            </div>
          </div>
        </header>

        <div className="mytickets-content">
          <section className="mytickets-heading">
            <div>
              <span className="mytickets-eyebrow">YOUR DIGITAL PASSES</span>
              <h1>My Tickets</h1>
              <p>
                Everything you need for your next event, right here.
              </p>
            </div>

            <button
              className="mytickets-booking-link"
              onClick={() => navigate("/user/bookings")}
            >
              View Bookings <ArrowUpRight size={16} />
            </button>
          </section>

          <div className="mytickets-preview-notice">
            <ShieldCheck size={17} />
            <span>
              This is a ticket preview. Your actual booking QR codes will
              appear after backend integration.
            </span>
          </div>

          <section className="mytickets-ticket-section">
            <div className="mytickets-section-heading">
              <div>
                <span>READY FOR YOUR NEXT EXPERIENCE</span>
                <h2>Your Digital Ticket</h2>
              </div>
              <span className="mytickets-count">
                {previewTickets.length} ticket
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="mytickets-ticket-selector">
              {previewTickets.map((ticket) => (
                <button
                  key={ticket.id}
                  className={`mytickets-selector-item ${
                    selectedTicket?.id === ticket.id ? "selected" : ""
                  }`}
                  onClick={() => setSelectedTicket(ticket)}
                >
                  <div className="mytickets-selector-icon">
                    <Ticket size={19} />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <strong>{ticket.title}</strong>
                    <span>{ticket.date}</span>
                  </div>
                  <CheckCircle2 size={18} />
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            {selectedTicket && (
              <div className="mytickets-ticket-wrapper">
                <div className="mytickets-ticket">
                  <div className="mytickets-ticket-main">
                    <div className="mytickets-ticket-top">
                      <div className="mytickets-ticket-brand">
                        <div className="mytickets-ticket-logo">E</div>
                        <div>
                          <strong>EventEase</strong>
                          <span>ADMIT ONE</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <span className="mytickets-confirmed">
                        <CheckCircle2 size={14} />
                        CONFIRMED
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mytickets-ticket-event">
                      <span>{selectedTicket.category.toUpperCase()}</span>
                      <h2>{selectedTicket.title}</h2>
                      <p>
                        Your entry pass to an experience worth remembering.
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mytickets-ticket-details">
                      <div>
                        <span>DATE</span>
                        <strong>{selectedTicket.date}</strong>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <span>TIME</span>
                        <strong>{selectedTicket.time}</strong>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <span>VENUE</span>
                        <strong>{selectedTicket.venue}</strong>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <span>LOCATION</span>
                        <strong>{selectedTicket.location}</strong>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <span>TICKET TYPE</span>
                        <strong>{selectedTicket.ticketType}</strong>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <span>QUANTITY</span>
                        <strong>{selectedTicket.quantity}</strong>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mytickets-ticket-bottom">
                      <div>
                        <span>ATTENDEE</span>
                        <strong>{user?.name || selectedTicket.attendee}</strong>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <span>BOOKING ID</span>
                        <strong>{selectedTicket.bookingId}</strong>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="mytickets-ticket-stub">
                    <div className="mytickets-stub-heading">
                      <span>YOUR ENTRY PASS</span>
                      <QrCode size={19} />
                    </div>

                    <div className="mytickets-qr-placeholder">
                      <QrCode size={105} strokeWidth={1.3} />
                      <span>QR PREVIEW</span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mytickets-stub-id">
                      <span>TICKET ID</span>
                      <strong>{selectedTicket.id}</strong>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mytickets-stub-price">
                      <span>PAID AMOUNT</span>
                      <strong>₹{selectedTicket.price}</strong>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mytickets-stub-note">
                      <ShieldCheck size={15} />
                      Keep this ticket ready at entry.
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="mytickets-ticket-actions">
                  <button
                    className="mytickets-print-button"
                    onClick={handlePrint}
                  >
                    <Printer size={17} />
                    Print Ticket
                  </button>

                  <button
                    className="mytickets-download-button"
                    onClick={handlePrint}
                  >
                    <Download size={17} />
                    Save as PDF
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </section>

          <section className="mytickets-reminder">
            <div className="mytickets-reminder-icon">
              <Clock size={21} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3>A little reminder before you go</h3>
              <p>
                Arrive early, keep your ticket accessible, and have your
                booking details ready at the entrance.
              </p>
            </div>
          </section>

          <footer className="mytickets-footer">
            <span>© 2026 EventEase. Made for experiences.</span>
            <span>Your next story starts here.</span>
          </footer>
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

export default MyTickets;

import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  CalendarDays,
  Plus,
  Users,
  BarChart3,
  Ticket,
  TrendingUp,
  IndianRupee,
  UserCheck,
  ArrowUpRight,
  ArrowDownRight,
  Menu,
  X,
  LogOut,
  Download,
  Activity,
  MapPin
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import "./OrganizerAnalytics.css";

const monthlyData = [
  { month: "Jun", bookings: 35 },
  { month: "Jul", bookings: 52 },
  { month: "Aug", bookings: 44 },
  { month: "Sep", bookings: 68 },
  { month: "Oct", bookings: 82 },
  { month: "Nov", bookings: 96 }
];

const categoryData = [
  { name: "Technology", percentage: 42, color: "#087f5b" },
  { name: "Music", percentage: 25, color: "#c49a50" },
  { name: "Workshop", percentage: 18, color: "#e58b71" },
  { name: "Cultural", percentage: 15, color: "#64748b" }
];

const topEvents = [
  {
    name: "Tech Innovation Summit 2026",
    category: "Technology",
    bookings: 76,
    revenue: "₹15,124",
    attendance: "84%"
  },
  {
    name: "AI & Cybersecurity Summit",
    category: "Technology",
    bookings: 58,
    revenue: "₹11,542",
    attendance: "—"
  },
  {
    name: "Creative Design Workshop",
    category: "Workshop",
    bookings: 42,
    revenue: "₹8,400",
    attendance: "91%"
  }
];

function OrganizerAnalytics() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { logout } = useAuth();
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout();
    navigate("/login");
  };

  const maxBookings = Math.max(...monthlyData.map((item) => item.bookings));

  return (
    <div className="analytics-layout">
      <aside className={`analytics-sidebar ${menuOpen ? "open" : ""}`}>
        <div className="analytics-brand">
          <div className="analytics-brand-icon">E</div>
          <div>
            <h2>EventEase</h2>
            <span>ORGANIZER STUDIO</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="analytics-sidebar-label">WORKSPACE</div>

        <nav className="analytics-nav">
          <button onClick={() => navigate("/organizer")}>
            <LayoutDashboard size={19} />
            Overview
          </button>

          <button onClick={() => navigate("/organizer/events")}>
            <CalendarDays size={19} />
            My Events
          </button>

          <button onClick={() => navigate("/organizer/events/new")}>
            <Plus size={19} />
            Create Event
          </button>

          <button onClick={() => navigate("/organizer/attendees")}>
            <Users size={19} />
            Attendee Management
          </button>

          <button className="active">
            <BarChart3 size={19} />
            Analytics
          </button>
        </nav>

        <div className="analytics-sidebar-bottom">
          <div className="analytics-sidebar-note">
            <Activity size={18} />
            <p>Your event performance at a glance.</p>
          </div>

          <button className="analytics-logout" onClick={handleLogout}>
            <LogOut size={18} />
            Sign Out
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {menuOpen && (
        <div
          className="analytics-overlay"
          onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
        />
      )}

      <main className="analytics-main">
        <header className="analytics-topbar">
          <button
            className="analytics-menu-button"
            onClick={() => setMenuOpen(!menuOpen)}
          >
            {menuOpen ? <X size={22} /> : <Menu size={22} />}
          </button>

          <div>
            <span className="analytics-breadcrumb">Organizer /</span>
            <strong> Analytics</strong>
          </div>

          <div className="analytics-topbar-right">
            <span className="analytics-preview-label">
              PREVIEW DATA
            </span>
            <div className="analytics-avatar">O</div>
          </div>
        </header>

        <section className="analytics-content">
          <div className="analytics-heading">
            <div>
              <span className="analytics-eyebrow">PERFORMANCE OVERVIEW</span>
              <h1>Analytics & Insights</h1>
              <p>
                Understand your event performance and make better decisions.
              </p>
            </div>

            <button
              className="analytics-export-button"
              onClick={() => window.print()}
            >
              <Download size={17} />
              Export Report
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="analytics-notice">
            <Activity size={17} />

            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="analytics-stats-grid">

              </div>
