import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  Search,
  Users,
  Ticket,
  IndianRupee,
  CalendarDays,
  Download,
  Eye,
  ChevronDown
} from "lucide-react";
import "./OrganizerAttendees.css";

const previewEvents = [
  {
    id: "event-1",
    title: "Tech Innovation Summit 2026"
  },
  {
    id: "event-2",
    title: "AI & Cybersecurity Summit"
  }
];

// Illustrative preview records. Replace with API data during integration.
const previewAttendees = [
  {
    id: "BK-1001",
    name: "Aarav Sharma",
    email: "aarav@example.com",
    tickets: 2,
    amount: 398,
    date: "15 Nov 2026",
    status: "Confirmed"
  },
  {
    id: "BK-1002",
    name: "Ananya Verma",
    email: "ananya@example.com",
    tickets: 1,
    amount: 199,
    date: "16 Nov 2026",
    status: "Confirmed"
  },
  {
    id: "BK-1003",
    name: "Rohan Gupta",
    email: "rohan@example.com",
    tickets: 3,
    amount: 597,
    date: "17 Nov 2026",
    status: "Pending"
  },
  {
    id: "BK-1004",
    name: "Meera Singh",
    email: "meera@example.com",
    tickets: 1,
    amount: 199,
    date: "18 Nov 2026",
    status: "Confirmed"
  },
  {
    id: "BK-1005",
    name: "Kabir Malhotra",
    email: "kabir@example.com",
    tickets: 2,
    amount: 398,
    date: "19 Nov 2026",
    status: "Cancelled"
  }
];

export default function OrganizerAttendees() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [selectedEvent, setSelectedEvent] = useState("event-1");
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState("All");
  const [selectedAttendee, setSelectedAttendee] = useState(null);

  const selectedEventName =
    previewEvents.find((event) => event.id === selectedEvent)?.title || "";

  const filteredAttendees = useMemo(() => {
    return previewAttendees.filter((attendee) => {
      const matchesSearch =
        attendee.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
        attendee.email.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
        attendee.id.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase());

      const matchesStatus =
        statusFilter === "All" || attendee.status === statusFilter;

      return matchesSearch && matchesStatus;
    });
  }, [search, statusFilter]);

  const confirmed = previewAttendees.filter(
    (attendee) => attendee.status === "Confirmed"
  );

  const totalTickets = confirmed.reduce(
    (sum, attendee) => sum + attendee.tickets,
    0
  );

  const totalRevenue = confirmed.reduce(
    (sum, attendee) => sum + attendee.amount,
    0
  );

  const handleExport = () => {
    const headers = [
      "Booking ID",
      "Name",
      "Email",
      "Tickets",
      "Amount",
      "Booking Date",
      "Status"
    ];

    const rows = filteredAttendees.map((attendee) => [
      attendee.id,
      attendee.name,
      attendee.email,
      attendee.tickets,
      attendee.amount,
      attendee.date,
      attendee.status
    ]);

    const csv = [headers, ...rows]
      .map((row) =>
        row.map((value) => `"${String(value).replace(/"/g, '""')}"`).join(",")
      )
      .join("\n");

    const blob = new Blob([csv], {
      type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8;"
    });

    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement("a");

    link.href = url;
    link.download = "eventease-attendees-preview.csv";
    link.click();

    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  return (
    <div className="attendees-page">

      <header className="attendees-header">
        <button
          className="attendees-back"
          onClick={() => navigate("/organizer")}
        >
          <ArrowLeft size={17} />
          Dashboard
        </button>

        <span>EVENTEASE / ORGANIZER STUDIO</span>
      </header>

      <main className="attendees-container">

        <section className="attendees-title-section">
          <div>
            <span className="attendees-eyebrow">
              EVENT MANAGEMENT
            </span>

            <h1>Attendee Management</h1>

            <p>
              Know your audience, manage registrations and track attendance.
            </p>
          </div>

          <button
            className="attendees-export"
            onClick={handleExport}
          >
            <Download size={16} />
            Export CSV
          </button>
        </section>

        <section className="attendees-event-selector">
          <div>
            <span>SELECT EVENT</span>
            <h3>{selectedEventName}</h3>
          </div>

          <div className="attendees-select-wrap">
            <select
              value={selectedEvent}
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedEvent(e.target.value)}
            >
              {previewEvents.map((event) => (
                <option key={event.id} value={event.id}>
                  {event.title}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
            <ChevronDown size={16} />
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="attendees-stats">

          <article className="attendee-stat-card">
            <div className="attendee-stat-icon">
              <Users size={20} />
            </div>
            <span>Total Registrations</span>
            <strong>{previewAttendees.length}</strong>
            <small>All booking records</small>
          </article>

          <article className="attendee-stat-card">
            <div className="attendee-stat-icon">
              <Ticket size={20} />
            </div>
            <span>Confirmed Tickets</span>
            <strong>{totalTickets}</strong>
            <small>Successfully booked</small>
          </article>

          <article className="attendee-stat-card">
            <div className="attendee-stat-icon">
              <IndianRupee size={20} />
            </div>
            <span>Booking Revenue</span>
            <strong>₹{totalRevenue.toLocaleString("en-IN")}</strong>
            <small>From confirmed bookings</small>
          </article>

          <article className="attendee-stat-card">
            <div className="attendee-stat-icon">
              <CalendarDays size={20} />
            </div>
            <span>Pending Bookings</span>
            <strong>
              {previewAttendees.filter(
                (attendee) => attendee.status === "Pending"
              ).length}
            </strong>
            <small>Awaiting confirmation</small>
          </article>

        </section>

        <section className="attendees-table-section">

          <div className="attendees-table-heading">
            <div>
              <h2>Registered Attendees</h2>
              <p>View and manage event booking records.</p>
            </div>

            <span className="attendees-count">
              {filteredAttendees.length} records
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="attendees-toolbar">

            <div className="attendees-search">
              <Search size={17} />
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="Search name, email or booking ID..."
                value={search}
                onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
              />
            </div>

            <select
              className="attendees-filter"
              value={statusFilter}
              onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)}
            >
              <option value="All">All statuses</option>
              <option value="Confirmed">Confirmed</option>
              <option value="Pending">Pending</option>
              <option value="Cancelled">Cancelled</option>
            </select>

          </div>

          <div className="attendees-preview-notice">
            Preview data only — actual attendee records will appear after backend integration.
          </div>

          <div className="attendees-table-wrapper">
            <table className="attendees-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>ATTENDEE</th>
                  <th>BOOKING ID</th>
                  <th>TICKETS</th>
                  <th>AMOUNT</th>
                  <th>BOOKED ON</th>
                  <th>STATUS</th>
                  <th>DETAILS</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>

              <tbody>
                {filteredAttendees.map((attendee) => (
                  <tr key={attendee.id}>
                    <td>
                      <div className="attendee-person">
                        <div className="attendee-avatar">
                          {attendee.name.charAt(0)}
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <strong>{attendee.name}</strong>
                          <span>{attendee.email}</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </td>

                    <td className="attendee-booking-id">
                      {attendee.id}
                    </td>

                    <td>{attendee.tickets}</td>

                    <td>₹{attendee.amount}</td>

                    <td>{attendee.date}</td>

                    <td>
                      <span
                        className={`attendee-status ${attendee.status.toLowerCase()}`}
                      >
                        {attendee.status}
                      </span>
                    </td>

                    <td>
                      <button
                        className="attendee-view-button"
                        title="View attendee details"
                        onClick={() => setSelectedAttendee(attendee)}
                      >
                        <Eye size={17} />
                      </button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}

                {filteredAttendees.length === 0 && (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan="7" className="attendees-no-results">
                      No attendees match your search.
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

        </section>

        {selectedAttendee && (
          <div
            className="attendee-modal-overlay"
            onClick={() => setSelectedAttendee(null)}
          >
            <div
              className="attendee-modal"
              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            >
              <div className="attendee-modal-heading">
                <h2>Booking Details</h2>
                <button onClick={() => setSelectedAttendee(null)}>
                  ×
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="attendee-modal-avatar">
                {selectedAttendee.name.charAt(0)}
              </div>

              <h3>{selectedAttendee.name}</h3>
              <p>{selectedAttendee.email}</p>

              <div className="attendee-detail-list">
                <div>
                  <span>Booking ID</span>
                  <strong>{selectedAttendee.id}</strong>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>Event</span>
                  <strong>{selectedEventName}</strong>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>Tickets</span>
                  <strong>{selectedAttendee.tickets}</strong>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>Amount</span>
                  <strong>₹{selectedAttendee.amount}</strong>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>Booking Date</span>
                  <strong>{selectedAttendee.date}</strong>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>Status</span>
                  <strong>{selectedAttendee.status}</strong>
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                className="attendee-modal-close"
                onClick={() => setSelectedAttendee(null)}
              >
                Close
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <footer className="attendees-footer">
          EVENTEASE ORGANIZER STUDIO
          <span>Creating memorable experiences.</span>
        </footer>

      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend\src\pages\OrganizerDashboard.jsx
==================================================

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  CalendarDays,
  Plus,
  Users,
  TrendingUp,
  Ticket,
  MapPin,
  Clock,
  Pencil,
  Trash2,
  LogOut,
  RefreshCw,
  Menu,
  X,
  CheckCircle,
  AlertCircle,
  XCircle
} from "lucide-react";

import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import api from "../services/api";
import "./OrganizerDashboard.css";

export default function OrganizerDashboard() {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [events, setEvents] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  // Fetch organizer events
  const fetchEvents = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      const response = await api.get("/events/my-events");
      const data = response.data;

      setEvents(
        Array.isArray(data)
          ? data
          : data.events || data.data?.events || []
      );
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message || "Unable to load your events."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchEvents();
  }, []);

  // Delete event
  const handleDelete = async (eventId) => {
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      "Are you sure you want to delete this event?"
    );

    if (!confirmed) return;

    try {
      await api.delete(`/events/${eventId}`);

      setEvents((previous) =>
        previous.filter((event) => event._id !== eventId)
      );
    } catch (err) {
      alert(
        err.response?.data?.message || "Could not delete event."
      );
    }
  };

  // Logout
  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await logout();
    navigate("/login");
  };

  // Dashboard statistics
  const approved = events.filter(
    (event) => event.approvalStatus === "approved"
  ).length;

  const pending = events.filter(
    (event) => event.approvalStatus === "pending"
  ).length;

  const totalCapacity = events.reduce(
    (sum, event) => sum + Number(event.capacity || 0),
    0
  );

  // Event approval status
  const getStatus = (status) => {
    if (status === "approved") {
      return {
        label: "Approved",
        icon: CheckCircle,
        className: "approved"
      };
    }

    if (status === "rejected") {
      return {
        label: "Rejected",
        icon: XCircle,
        className: "rejected"
      };
    }

    return {
      label: "Pending review",
      icon: AlertCircle,
      className: "pending"
    };
  };

  // Format event date
  const formatDate = (date) => {
    if (!date) return "Date not set";

    return new Date(date).toLocaleDateString("en-IN", {
      day: "numeric",
      month: "short",
      year: "numeric"
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="organizer-layout">

      {/* Sidebar */}
      <aside className={`organizer-sidebar ${menuOpen ? "open" : ""}`}>

        <div className="organizer-brand">
          <div className="organizer-brand-icon">E</div>

          <div>
            <h2>EventEase</h2>
            <span>ORGANIZER STUDIO</span>
          </div>

          <button
            className="organizer-close"
            onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
          >
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="organizer-side-label">
          WORKSPACE
        </div>

        <button
          className="organizer-nav-item active"
          onClick={() => {
            navigate("/organizer");
            setMenuOpen(false);
          }}
        >
          <LayoutDashboard size={19} />
          Overview
        </button>

        <button
          className="organizer-nav-item"
          onClick={() => {
            navigate("/organizer/events");
            setMenuOpen(false);
          }}
        >
          <CalendarDays size={19} />
          My Events
        </button>

        <button
          className="organizer-nav-item"
          onClick={() => {
            navigate("/organizer/attendees");
            setMenuOpen(false);
          }}
        >
          <Users size={19} />
          Attendee Management
        </button>

        <button
          className="organizer-nav-item"
          onClick={() => {
            navigate("/organizer/analytics");
            setMenuOpen(false);
          }}
        >
          <TrendingUp size={19} />
          Analytics
        </button>

        <button
          className="organizer-nav-item"
          onClick={() => {
            navigate("/organizer/events/new");
            setMenuOpen(false);
          }}
        >
          <Plus size={19} />
          Create Event
        </button>

        <div className="organizer-side-bottom">

          <div className="organizer-profile">
            <div className="organizer-avatar">
              {user?.name?.charAt(0)?.toUpperCase() || "O"}
            </div>

            <div className="organizer-profile-info">
              <strong>{user?.name || "Organizer"}</strong>
              <span>Verified Organizer</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            className="organizer-logout"
            onClick={handleLogout}
          >
            <LogOut size={18} />
            Sign out
          </button>

        </div>
      </aside>

      {/* Mobile overlay */}
      {menuOpen && (
        <div
          className="organizer-overlay"
          onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
        />
      )}

      {/* Main content */}
      <main className="organizer-main">

        <header className="organizer-topbar">

          <button
            className="organizer-menu-button"
            onClick={() => setMenuOpen(true)}
          >
            <Menu size={22} />
          </button>

          <div>
            <span className="organizer-top-label">
              EVENT MANAGEMENT
            </span>
            <h2>Organizer Dashboard</h2>
          </div>

          <div className="organizer-top-actions">

            <button
              className="organizer-refresh"
              onClick={fetchEvents}
              disabled={loading}
            >
              <RefreshCw size={16} />
              Refresh
            </button>

            <button
              className="organizer-create-button"
              onClick={() => navigate("/organizer/events/new")}
            >
              <Plus size={18} />
              Create Event
            </button>

          </div>
        </header>

        <section className="organizer-content">

          {/* Welcome */}
          <div className="organizer-welcome">

            <div>
              <span className="organizer-eyebrow">
                YOUR EVENT SPACE
              </span>

              <h1>
                Welcome back, {user?.name?.split(" ")[0] || "Organizer"}.
              </h1>

              <p>
                Manage your events, monitor approvals and grow your audience.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="organizer-welcome-mark">
              E.
            </div>

          </div>

          {/* Statistics */}
          <div className="organizer-section-heading">
            <div>
              <h2>Performance overview</h2>
              <p>A snapshot of your event activity</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="organizer-stats-grid">

            <div className="organizer-stat-card">
              <div className="organizer-stat-top">
                <span>Total Events</span>
                <CalendarDays size={20} />
              </div>
              <strong>{events.length}</strong>
              <small>Events created by you</small>
            </div>

            <div className="organizer-stat-card">
              <div className="organizer-stat-top">
                <span>Approved</span>
                <CheckCircle size={20} />
              </div>
              <strong>{approved}</strong>
              <small>Published events</small>
            </div>

            <div className="organizer-stat-card">
              <div className="organizer-stat-top">
                <span>Pending</span>
                <Clock size={20} />
              </div>
              <strong>{pending}</strong>
              <small>Awaiting admin review</small>
            </div>

            <div className="organizer-stat-card">
              <div className="organizer-stat-top">
                <span>Total Capacity</span>
                <Users size={20} />
              </div>
              <strong>{totalCapacity}</strong>
              <small>Total event capacity</small>
            </div>

          </div>

          {/* Recent events */}
          <div className="organizer-section-heading organizer-events-heading">

            <div>
              <h2>Your events</h2>
              <p>Manage and monitor your event submissions</p>
            </div>

            <button
              className="organizer-view-all"
              onClick={() => navigate("/organizer/events")}
            >
              View all
            </button>

          </div>

          {/* Error message */}
          {error && (
            <div className="organizer-error">
              <AlertCircle size={18} />
              {error}
              <button onClick={fetchEvents}>
                Try again
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Loading state */}
          {loading ? (

            <div className="organizer-loading">
              <RefreshCw
                className="organizer-spinner"
                size={24}
              />
              <p>Loading your events...</p>
            </div>

          ) : events.length === 0 ? (

            /* Empty state */
            <div className="organizer-empty">

              <div className="organizer-empty-icon">
                <CalendarDays size={32} />
              </div>

              <h3>Your event journey starts here.</h3>

              <p>
                Create your first event and submit it for admin approval.
              </p>

              <button
                className="organizer-create-button"
                onClick={() => navigate("/organizer/events/new")}
              >
                <Plus size={18} />
                Create your first event
              </button>

            </div>

          ) : (

            /* Events list */
            <div className="organizer-events-list">

              {events.slice(0, 5).map((event) => {

                const status = getStatus(event.approvalStatus);
                const StatusIcon = status.icon;

                return (
                  <article
                    className="organizer-event-row"
                    key={event._id}
                  >

                    <div className="organizer-event-icon">
                      <CalendarDays size={24} />
                    </div>

                    <div className="organizer-event-info">

                      <span className="organizer-event-category">
                        {event.category}
                      </span>

                      <h3>{event.title}</h3>

                      <div className="organizer-event-meta">

                        <span>
                          <Clock size={14} />
                          {formatDate(event.startDate)}
                        </span>

                        <span>
                          <MapPin size={14} />
                          {event.location || event.venue}
                        </span>

                        <span>
                          <Ticket size={14} />
                          ₹{event.ticketPrice}
                        </span>

                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className={`organizer-status ${status.className}`}>
                      <StatusIcon size={15} />
                      {status.label}
                    </div>

                    <div className="organizer-event-actions">

                      <button
                        title="Edit event"
                        onClick={() =>
                          navigate(`/organizer/events/${event._id}/edit`)
                        }
                      >
                        <Pencil size={17} />
                      </button>

                      <button
                        title="Delete event"
                        onClick={() => handleDelete(event._id)}
                      >
                        <Trash2 size={17} />
                      </button>

                    </div>

                  </article>
                );
              })}

            </div>
          )}

          <div className="organizer-footer">
            <span>EVENTEASE ORGANIZER STUDIO</span>
            <span>Made for memorable experiences.</span>
          </div>

        </section>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}



==================================================
FILE: frontend\src\pages\Register.jsx
==================================================

import { useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { CalendarDays, User, Mail, Lock, Eye, EyeOff } from "lucide-react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";

export default function Register() {
  const { register } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    name: "",
    email: "",
    password: ""
  });

  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError("");

    if (form.password.length < 8) {
      setError("Password must contain at least 8 characters.");
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);

    try {
      await register(form.name, form.email, form.password);
      navigate("/events");
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.message || "Registration failed.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="auth-page">
      <div className="auth-card">
        <Link to="/" className="auth-brand">
          <CalendarDays size={30} />
          <span>Event<span className="brand-accent">Ease</span></span>
        </Link>

        <h1>Create your account</h1>
        <p className="auth-subtitle">Join EventEase and find your next experience.</p>

        {error && <div className="error-message">{error}</div>}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <label>Full name</label>
          <div className="input-wrapper">
            <User size={19} />
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Your name"
              value={form.name}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })}
              minLength={2}
              maxLength={60}
              required
            />
          </div>

          <label>Email address</label>
          <div className="input-wrapper">
            <Mail size={19} />
            <input
              type="email"
              placeholder="you@example.com"
              value={form.email}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, email: e.target.value })}
              required
            />
          </div>

          <label>Password</label>
          <div className="input-wrapper">
            <Lock size={19} />
            <input
              type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
              placeholder="At least 8 characters"
              value={form.password}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, password: e.target.value })}
              minLength={8}
              required
            />
            <button
              type="button"
              className="password-toggle"
              onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
            >
              {showPassword ? <EyeOff size={19} /> : <Eye size={19} />}
            </button>
          </div>

          <button className="auth-submit" disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? "Creating account..." : "Create Account"}
          </button>
        </form>

        <p className="auth-footer">
          Already have an account? <Link to="/login">Sign in</Link>
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend\src\pages\SavedEvents.jsx
==================================================

import React, { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  Search, Heart, CalendarDays, MapPin, ArrowRight,
  Trash2, Ticket, Compass, LayoutDashboard
} from "lucide-react";
import "./SavedEvents.css";

const previewEvents = [
  {
    _id: "saved-1",
    title: "Tech Innovation Summit 2026",
    category: "Technology",
    date: "December 15, 2026",
    time: "10:00 AM – 5:00 PM",
    location: "SRMU Campus Auditorium, Lucknow",
    price: 199,
    image: "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1540575467063-178a50c2df87?w=900",
    description: "Explore AI, cybersecurity, cloud computing and emerging technologies."
  },
  {
    _id: "saved-2",
    title: "Music Under The Stars",
    category: "Music",
    date: "December 22, 2026",
    time: "6:00 PM – 10:00 PM",
    location: "Gomti Riverfront, Lucknow",
    price: 499,
    image: "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1459749411175-04bf5292ceea?w=900",
    description: "An unforgettable evening of live music and performances."
  },
  {
    _id: "saved-3",
    title: "Creative Minds Workshop",
    category: "Workshop",
    date: "January 08, 2027",
    time: "11:00 AM – 3:00 PM",
    location: "Innovation Centre, Lucknow",
    price: 299,
    image: "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1516321318423-f06f85e504b3?w=900",
    description: "A hands-on workshop for creative thinking and innovation."
  },
  {
    _id: "saved-4",
    title: "Startup & Business Connect",
    category: "Business",
    date: "January 18, 2027",
    time: "9:30 AM – 4:00 PM",
    location: "Hotel Renaissance, Lucknow",
    price: 349,
    image: "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1511578314322-379afb476865?w=900",
    description: "Connect with entrepreneurs, founders and industry professionals."
  }
];

const categories = [
  "All", "Technology", "Music", "Workshop", "Business"
];

export default function SavedEvents() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [events, setEvents] = useState(previewEvents);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("All");

  const filteredEvents = useMemo(() => {
    return events.filter((event) => {
      const matchesSearch =
        event.title.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
        event.location.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase());

      const matchesCategory =
        category === "All" || event.category === category;

      return matchesSearch && matchesCategory;
    });
  }, [events, search, category]);

  const removeEvent = (id) => {
    setEvents((current) =>
      current.filter((event) => event._id !== id)
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="saved-page">
      <aside className="saved-sidebar">
        <div className="saved-brand" onClick={() => navigate("/events")}>
          <span className="saved-brand-icon">E</span>
          <span>event<span>ease</span></span>
        </div>

        <p className="saved-nav-label">MENU</p>

        <button onClick={() => navigate("/dashboard")}>
          <LayoutDashboard size={18} /> Dashboard
        </button>
        <button onClick={() => navigate("/events")}>
          <Compass size={18} /> Explore Events
        </button>
        <button className="active">
          <Heart size={18} /> Saved Events
        </button>
        <button onClick={() => navigate("/user/bookings")}>
          <Ticket size={18} /> My Bookings
        </button>

        <div className="saved-sidebar-bottom">
          <p>Your next great experience is just around the corner.</p>
          <button onClick={() => navigate("/events")}>
            Discover events <ArrowRight size={15} />
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <main className="saved-main">
        <header className="saved-topbar">
          <div>
            <span className="saved-eyebrow">YOUR COLLECTION</span>
            <h1>Saved <span>Events.</span></h1>
            <p>Keep the experiences you love, all in one place.</p>
          </div>
          <button
            className="saved-explore-btn"
            onClick={() => navigate("/events")}
          >
            Explore events <ArrowRight size={16} />
          </button>
        </header>

        <section className="saved-summary">
          <div className="saved-summary-icon">
            <Heart size={23} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <strong>{events.length}</strong>
            <span>Events saved</span>
          </div>
          <div className="saved-summary-note">
            Your personal shortlist of experiences
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="saved-controls">
          <div className="saved-search">
            <Search size={18} />
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Search saved events or locations..."
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="saved-categories">
            {categories.map((item) => (
              <button
                key={item}
                className={category === item ? "selected" : ""}
                onClick={() => setCategory(item)}
              >
                {item}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="saved-events-section">
          <div className="saved-section-heading">
            <div>
              <h2>Your wishlist</h2>
              <p>{filteredEvents.length} events found</p>
            </div>
            <span className="saved-preview-label">PREVIEW DATA</span>
          </div>

          {filteredEvents.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="saved-events-grid">
              {filteredEvents.map((event) => (
                <article className="saved-event-card" key={event._id}>
                  <div className="saved-event-image">
                    <img src={event.image} alt={event.title} />
                    <span className="saved-event-category">
                      {event.category}
                    </span>
                    <button
                      className="saved-remove-btn"
                      onClick={() => removeEvent(event._id)}
                      aria-label={`Remove ${event.title} from saved events`}
                      title="Remove from saved events"
                    >
                      <Heart size={18} fill="currentColor" />
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <div className="saved-event-content">
                    <h3>{event.title}</h3>
                    <p className="saved-event-description">
                      {event.description}
                    </p>

                    <div className="saved-event-info">
                      <span><CalendarDays size={15} /> {event.date}</span>
                      <span><MapPin size={15} /> {event.location}</span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="saved-event-footer">
                      <div className="saved-event-price">
                        <small>Starting from</small>
                        <strong>₹{event.price}</strong>
                      </div>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => navigate(`/events/${event._id}`)}
                      >
                        View event <ArrowRight size={15} />
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    <button
                      className="saved-remove-text"
                      onClick={() => removeEvent(event._id)}
                    >
                      <Trash2 size={14} /> Remove from wishlist
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </article>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="saved-empty">
              <div><Heart size={32} /></div>
              <h3>No saved events found</h3>
              <p>
                {events.length === 0
                  ? "You haven't saved any events yet. Explore and find something exciting."
                  : "Try changing your search or category filter."}
              </p>
              <button onClick={() => navigate("/events")}>
                Explore events <ArrowRight size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </section>

        <footer className="saved-footer">
          <span>EVENTEASE © 2026</span>
          <span>Make every moment an experience.</span>
        </footer>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  Compass,
  Ticket,
  CalendarDays,
  Heart,
  UserRound,
  LogOut,
  Menu,
  X,
  ArrowUpRight,
  MapPin,
  Clock,
  Search,
  ChevronRight,
  Sparkles,
  Bookmark,
  QrCode
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import "./UserDashboard.css";

const upcomingBookings = [
  {
    id: "EVT-2026-001",
    title: "Tech Innovation Summit 2026",
    category: "Technology",
    date: "15 December 2026",
    time: "10:00 AM – 5:00 PM",
    venue: "SRMU Campus Auditorium",
    location: "Lucknow",
    ticket: "1 Ticket",
    status: "Confirmed",
    color: "emerald"
  }
];

const recommendedEvents = [
  {
    id: 1,
    title: "Future of Artificial Intelligence",
    category: "Technology",
    date: "22 December 2026",
    location: "Lucknow",
    price: "₹299",
    image:
      "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1485827404703-89b55fcc595e?w=800"
  },
  {
    id: 2,
    title: "The Creative Minds Workshop",
    category: "Workshop",
    date: "28 December 2026",
    location: "Lucknow",
    price: "₹199",
    image:
      "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1516321318423-f06f85e504b3?w=800"
  },
  {
    id: 3,
    title: "The Grand Music Evening",
    category: "Music",
    date: "05 January 2027",
    location: "Lucknow",
    price: "₹499",
    image:
      "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1501386761578-eac5c94b800a?w=800"
  }
];

function UserDashboard() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  const firstName = user?.name?.split(" ")[0] || "there";

  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout();
    navigate("/login");
  };

  return (
    <div className="user-dashboard-layout">
      <aside className={`user-sidebar ${menuOpen ? "open" : ""}`}>
        <div className="user-brand">
          <div className="user-brand-icon">E</div>
          <div>
            <h2>EventEase</h2>
            <span>YOUR EVENT WORLD</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="user-sidebar-label">MENU</div>

        <nav className="user-navigation">
          <button className="active">
            <LayoutDashboard size={19} />
            Dashboard
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => {
              navigate("/events");
              setMenuOpen(false);
            }}
          >
            <Compass size={19} />
            Explore Events
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => {
              navigate("/user/bookings");
              setMenuOpen(false);
            }}
          >
            <Ticket size={19} />
            My Bookings
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => {
              navigate("/user/tickets");
              setMenuOpen(false);
            }}
          >
            <QrCode size={19} />
            My Tickets
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => {
              navigate("/user/saved");
              setMenuOpen(false);
            }}
          >
            <Heart size={19} />
            Saved Events
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => {
              navigate("/user/profile");
              setMenuOpen(false);
            }}
          >
            <UserRound size={19} />
            My Profile
          </button>
        </nav>

        <div className="user-sidebar-bottom">
          <div className="user-sidebar-promo">
            <Sparkles size={19} />
            <h4>Make memories.</h4>
            <p>Discover experiences worth remembering.</p>
            <button onClick={() => navigate("/events")}>
              Explore now <ArrowUpRight size={14} />
            </button>
          </div>

          <button className="user-logout" onClick={handleLogout}>
            <LogOut size={18} />
            Sign Out
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {menuOpen && (
        <div
          className="user-sidebar-overlay"
          onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
        />
      )}

      <main className="user-dashboard-main">
        <header className="user-topbar">
          <div className="user-topbar-left">
            <button
              className="user-menu-toggle"
              onClick={() => setMenuOpen(!menuOpen)}
            >
              {menuOpen ? <X size={22} /> : <Menu size={22} />}
            </button>
            <span>My Space</span>
            <ChevronRight size={15} />
            <strong>Dashboard</strong>
          </div>

          <div className="user-topbar-right">
            <span className="user-member-label">EVENT ENTHUSIAST</span>
            <div className="user-avatar">
              {firstName.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
            </div>
          </div>
        </header>

        <div className="user-dashboard-content">
          <section className="user-welcome-section">
            <div>
              <span className="user-eyebrow">YOUR EVENT JOURNEY</span>
              <h1>
                Welcome back, <span>{firstName}.</span>
              </h1>
              <p>
                Your next memorable experience could be just around the
                corner.
              </p>
            </div>

            <button
              className="user-explore-button"
              onClick={() => navigate("/events")}
            >
              <Search size={17} />
              Explore Events
            </button>
          </section>

          <section className="user-hero-banner">
            <div className="user-hero-copy">
              <span>DISCOVER. CONNECT. EXPERIENCE.</span>
              <h2>Life is better when you're part of something.</h2>
              <p>
                Find experiences, meet people, and make every moment count.
              </p>
              <button onClick={() => navigate("/events")}>
                Find your next event <ArrowUpRight size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="user-hero-decoration">
              <CalendarDays size={110} strokeWidth={0.8} />
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="user-overview-stats">
            <div className="user-overview-card">
              <div className="user-overview-icon green">
                <Ticket size={20} />
              </div>
              <div>
                <span>Total Bookings</span>
                <h2>2</h2>
                <small>Across all events</small>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="user-overview-card">
              <div className="user-overview-icon gold">
                <CalendarDays size={20} />
              </div>
              <div>
                <span>Upcoming Events</span>
                <h2>2</h2>

            </div>

            <div className="user-overview-card">

              </div>
              <div>
                <span>Saved Events</span>
                <h2>4</h2>
                <small>On your wishlist</small>
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="user-bookings-section">
            <div className="user-section-heading">
              <div>
                <span className="user-section-eyebrow">YOUR CALENDAR</span>
                <h2>Upcoming Bookings</h2>
                <p>Keep track of the experiences you've booked.</p>
              </div>

              <button
                className="user-text-link"
                onClick={() => navigate("/user/bookings")}
              >
                View all <ArrowUpRight size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="user-bookings-list">
              {upcomingBookings.map((booking) => (
                <article className="user-booking-card" key={booking.id}>
                  <div className={`user-booking-date ${booking.color}`}>
                    <CalendarDays size={22} />
                  </div>

                  <div className="user-booking-info">
                    <div className="user-booking-title-row">
                      <h3>{booking.title}</h3>
                      <span
                        className={`user-booking-status ${
                          booking.status === "Confirmed"
                            ? "confirmed"
                            : "pending"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {booking.status}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <span className="user-booking-category">
                      {booking.category}
                    </span>

                    <div className="user-booking-details">
                      <span>
                        <CalendarDays size={14} />
                        {booking.date}
                      </span>
                      <span>
                        <Clock size={14} />
                        {booking.time}
                      </span>
                      <span>
                        <MapPin size={14} />
                        {booking.venue}, {booking.location}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="user-booking-action">
                    <span>{booking.ticket}</span>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => navigate("/user/tickets")}
                      disabled={booking.status !== "Confirmed"}
                    >
                      View Ticket <ChevronRight size={15} />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </article>
              ))}
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="user-recommendations-section">
            <div className="user-section-heading">
              <div>
                <span className="user-section-eyebrow">HANDPICKED FOR YOU</span>
                <h2>Discover More Events</h2>
                <p>Find something exciting to look forward to.</p>
              </div>

              <button
                className="user-text-link"
                onClick={() => navigate("/events")}
              >
                Explore all <ArrowUpRight size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="user-recommendation-grid">
              {recommendedEvents.map((event) => (
                <article className="user-recommendation-card" key={event.id}>
                  <div className="user-recommendation-image">
                    <img src={event.image} alt={event.title} />
                    <span>{event.category}</span>
                    <button
                      className="user-save-event"
                      aria-label={`Save ${event.title}`}
                      onClick={() => navigate("/user/saved")}
                    >
                      <Bookmark size={17} />
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <div className="user-recommendation-info">
                    <h3>{event.title}</h3>
                    <span>
                      <CalendarDays size={14} />
                      {event.date}
                    </span>
                    <span>
                      <MapPin size={14} />
                      {event.location}
                    </span>

                    <div className="user-recommendation-bottom">

                    </div>

          <footer className="user-dashboard-footer">
            <span>© 2026 EventEase. Made for experiences.</span>
            <span>Your next story starts here.</span>
          </footer>
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

export default UserDashboard;

FILE: frontend\src\pages\UserProfile.jsx

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  Compass,
  Heart,
  Ticket,
  UserRound,
  Settings,
  LockKeyhole,
  Mail,
  Phone,
  MapPin,
  CalendarDays,
  Save,
  ArrowLeft,
  LogOut,
  Camera,
  ShieldCheck
} from "lucide-react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import "./UserProfile.css";

export default function UserProfile() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState("profile");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  const [profile, setProfile] = useState({
    name: user?.name || "",
    email: user?.email || "",
    phone: user?.phone || "",
    location: user?.location || "",
    bio: user?.bio || ""
  });

  const [passwords, setPasswords] = useState({
    currentPassword: "",
    newPassword: "",
    confirmPassword: ""
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    setProfile({
      name: user?.name || "",
      email: user?.email || "",
      phone: user?.phone || "",
      location: user?.location || "",
      bio: user?.bio || ""
    });
  }, [user]);

  const handleProfileChange = (e) => {
    const { name, value } = e.target;

    setProfile((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      [name]: value
    }));
  };

  const handlePasswordChange = (e) => {
    const { name, value } = e.target;

    setPasswords((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      [name]: value
    }));
  };

  const handleProfileSave = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setMessage(
      "Profile changes are ready. Backend integration is pending."
    );
  };

  const handlePasswordSave = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();

    if (passwords.newPassword.length < 8) {
      setMessage("Your new password must contain at least 8 characters.");
      return;
    }

    if (passwords.newPassword !== passwords.confirmPassword) {
      setMessage("New password and confirmation do not match.");
      return;
    }

    setMessage(
      "Password form validated. Backend integration is pending."
    );

    setPasswords({
      currentPassword: "",
      newPassword: "",
      confirmPassword: ""
    });
  };

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await logout();
    navigate("/login");
  };

  return (
    <div className="profile-layout">

      {/* Sidebar */}
      <aside className="profile-sidebar">

        <div className="profile-brand" onClick={() => navigate("/events")}>
          <div className="profile-brand-icon">E</div>
          <div>
            <h2>EventEase</h2>
            <span>YOUR EXPERIENCE HUB</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <span className="profile-nav-heading">MENU</span>

        <button onClick={() => navigate("/dashboard")}>
          <LayoutDashboard size={18} /> Dashboard
        </button>

        <button onClick={() => navigate("/events")}>
          <Compass size={18} /> Explore Events
        </button>

        <button onClick={() => navigate("/user/saved")}>
          <Heart size={18} /> Saved Events
        </button>

        <button onClick={() => navigate("/user/bookings")}>
          <Ticket size={18} /> My Bookings
        </button>

        <button onClick={() => navigate("/user/tickets")}>
          <CalendarDays size={18} /> My Tickets
        </button>

        <button className="active">
          <UserRound size={18} /> My Profile
        </button>

        <div className="profile-sidebar-bottom">
          <div className="profile-sidebar-user">
            <div className="profile-avatar-small">
              {user?.name?.charAt(0)?.toUpperCase() || "U"}
            </div>
            <div>
              <strong>{user?.name || "EventEase User"}</strong>
              <span>Attendee Account</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button className="profile-logout" onClick={handleLogout}>
            <LogOut size={17} /> Sign out
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {/* Main */}
      <main className="profile-main">

        <header className="profile-topbar">
          <div>
            <span className="profile-eyebrow">ACCOUNT SETTINGS</span>
            <h1>My Profile<span>.</span></h1>
            <p>Manage your personal information and account security.</p>
          </div>

          <button
            className="profile-back-btn"
            onClick={() => navigate("/dashboard")}
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Dashboard
          </button>
        </header>

        {/* Profile banner */}
        <section className="profile-hero">
          <div className="profile-hero-avatar">
            {profile.name?.charAt(0)?.toUpperCase() || "U"}
            <span><Camera size={15} /></span>
          </div>

          <div className="profile-hero-info">
            <h2>{profile.name || "Your Name"}</h2>
            <p>{profile.email}</p>
            <span className="profile-member-badge">
              <ShieldCheck size={14} /> Verified Account
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="profile-hero-mark">E.</div>
        </section>

        {/* Tabs */}
        <div className="profile-tabs">
          <button
            className={activeTab === "profile" ? "selected" : ""}
            onClick={() => {
              setActiveTab("profile");
              setMessage("");
            }}
          >
            <UserRound size={17} /> Personal Information
          </button>

          <button
            className={activeTab === "security" ? "selected" : ""}
            onClick={() => {
              setActiveTab("security");
              setMessage("");
            }}
          >
            <LockKeyhole size={17} /> Security
          </button>

          <button
            className={activeTab === "settings" ? "selected" : ""}
            onClick={() => {
              setActiveTab("settings");
              setMessage("");
            }}
          >
            <Settings size={17} /> Account
          </button>
        </div>

        {message && (
          <div className="profile-message">
            {message}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Personal information */}
        {activeTab === "profile" && (
          <section className="profile-panel">
            <div className="profile-panel-heading">
              <div>
                <h2>Personal Information</h2>
                <p>Update your personal details and contact information.</p>
              </div>
              <UserRound size={22} />
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleProfileSave}>
              <div className="profile-form-grid">

                <div className="profile-field">
                  <label>Full Name</label>
                  <div className="profile-input-wrap">
                    <UserRound size={17} />
                    <input
                      name="name"
                      value={profile.name}
                      onChange={handleProfileChange}
                      placeholder="Enter your full name"
                      required
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="profile-field">
                  <label>Email Address</label>
                  <div className="profile-input-wrap">
                    <Mail size={17} />
                    <input
                      type="email"
                      name="email"
                      value={profile.email}
                      readOnly
                    />
                  </div>
                  <small>Email editing is currently disabled.</small>
                </div>

                <div className="profile-field">
                  <label>Phone Number</label>
                  <div className="profile-input-wrap">
                    <Phone size={17} />
                    <input
                      name="phone"
                      value={profile.phone}
                      onChange={handleProfileChange}
                      placeholder="Enter phone number"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="profile-field">
                  <label>Location</label>
                  <div className="profile-input-wrap">
                    <MapPin size={17} />
                    <input
                      name="location"
                      value={profile.location}
                      onChange={handleProfileChange}
                      placeholder="City, State"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="profile-field full-width">
                  <label>About Me</label>
                  <textarea
                    name="bio"
                    value={profile.bio}
                    onChange={handleProfileChange}
                    placeholder="Tell us a little about yourself..."
                    rows={4}
                  />
                </div>

              </div>

              <div className="profile-form-actions">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="profile-cancel-btn"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setProfile({
                      name: user?.name || "",
                      email: user?.email || "",
                      phone: user?.phone || "",
                      location: user?.location || "",
                      bio: user?.bio || ""
                    });
                    setMessage("");
                  }}
                >
                  Reset
                </button>

                <button type="submit" className="profile-save-btn">
                  <Save size={17} /> Save Changes
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </section>
        )}

        {/* Security */}
        {activeTab === "security" && (
          <section className="profile-panel">
            <div className="profile-panel-heading">
              <div>
                <h2>Password & Security</h2>
                <p>Keep your EventEase account protected.</p>
              </div>
              <LockKeyhole size={22} />
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handlePasswordSave}>
              <div className="profile-security-note">
                <ShieldCheck size={21} />
                <div>
                  <strong>Account protection</strong>
                  <p>Choose a strong password with at least 8 characters.</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="profile-security-fields">

                <div className="profile-field">
                  <label>Current Password</label>
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    name="currentPassword"
                    value={passwords.currentPassword}
                    onChange={handlePasswordChange}
                    placeholder="Enter current password"
                    required
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="profile-field">
                  <label>New Password</label>
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    name="newPassword"
                    value={passwords.newPassword}
                    onChange={handlePasswordChange}
                    placeholder="Enter new password"
                    minLength={8}
                    required
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="profile-field">
                  <label>Confirm New Password</label>
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    name="confirmPassword"
                    value={passwords.confirmPassword}
                    onChange={handlePasswordChange}
                    placeholder="Confirm new password"
                    minLength={8}
                    required
                  />
                </div>

              </div>

              <div className="profile-form-actions">
                <button type="submit" className="profile-save-btn">
                  <LockKeyhole size={17} /> Update Password
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </section>
        )}

        {/* Account settings */}
        {activeTab === "settings" && (
          <section className="profile-panel">
            <div className="profile-panel-heading">
              <div>
                <h2>Account Settings</h2>
                <p>Review your account information and preferences.</p>
              </div>
              <Settings size={22} />
            </div>

            <div className="profile-account-list">

              <div className="profile-account-item">
                <div className="profile-account-icon">
                  <Mail size={19} />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <strong>Email Address</strong>
                  <span>{user?.email || "Not available"}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="profile-account-item">
                <div className="profile-account-icon">
                  <ShieldCheck size={19} />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <strong>Account Role</strong>
                  <span>{user?.role || "User"}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="profile-account-item">
                <div className="profile-account-icon">
                  <CalendarDays size={19} />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <strong>Account Status</strong>
                  <span>Active</span>
                </div>
              </div>

            </div>

            <div className="profile-danger-zone">
              <div>
                <h3>Sign out of EventEase</h3>
                <p>End your current session on this device.</p>
              </div>

              <button onClick={handleLogout}>
                <LogOut size={16} /> Sign out
              </button>
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        <footer className="profile-footer">
          <span>EVENTEASE © 2026</span>
          <span>Every experience begins with you.</span>
        </footer>

      </main>
    </div>
  );
}



==================================================
FILE: frontend\src\services\api.js
==================================================
import axios from "axios";

const api = axios.create({
  baseURL: import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || "http://localhost:5000/api",
  headers: {
    "Content-Type": "application/json"
  }
});

api.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
  const token = localStorage.getItem("eventease_token");

  if (token) {
    config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
  }

  return config;
});

export default api;
